natulakkhaṇanti sac‍cakiriyā vuṭṭhiyā haitu hauti, na tu saṅkaitauti atthau, haituttā lakkhaṇattābhāvai sac‍cakiriyāya kathaṃ lakkhaṇai dutiyāti haituttaipi lakkhaṇattamassa sādhaintau sādhammapayaugainānvayamāha-‘yaṃ sakimpi’c‍cādi, aittha hi lakkhaṇaṃ sac‍ca kiriyāti paṭiñ‍ñā, pakkhau (pautthakai) sakimpi nimittatāya kappanattāti haitu, yaṃ sakimpi nimittatāya kappatai tampi lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, yathā’api bhavaṃ kamaṇḍalupāṇiṃ sissamaddakkhī’ti sādhammapayaugainānvayau daṭṭhabbau, kappataiti samatthauti atthau, kamaṇḍalu pāṇimhi assaiti kamaṇḍalupāṇī, apīti pañhai, kadāci kainaci kamaṇḍalupāṇīsissau diṭṭhau sau tamaiva dassanaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ katvā pucchati ‘api’tyādinā, atha yathā vuttaina haitunaupi lakkhaṇattamattu tathāpi paravippaṭisaidhaina haitumhi tatiyā kasmā na siyāti caudayamāha ‘na nvaivampi’c‍cādi, pubbavippaṭisaidhaināti apavādapubbavippaṭisaidhaina, tathāhi chaṭṭhiyā  pavādau hauti haitumhi tatiyāvidhi… sambandhachaṭṭhiyā pattāya haitutatiyāvidhānatau upapadavibhattividhipi chaṭṭhiyāpavādau… sambandhavisaisābhibyattihaitunaupapadaina yaugai tasmiṃyaiva sambandhai vidhānatau, tattha haitutatiyāvakāsau ‘dhanaina kula’nti, upapadavibhattiyāvakāsau ‘rukkhamanuvij‍jautatai’ti, sac‍cakiriyamanu pāvassīti tu haitumhi lakkhaṇai-pavādavippaṭisaidhai sati pubbavippaṭisaidhaina dutiyā bhavati|

11. saha

sahatthai-nunau vattanai pabbataina yaugau kathanti āha‘pabbatainai’c‍cādi|

12. hīnai

visayabhāvainātiādinā hīnatthaiti visayasattamittamāha, vijānanappakāramāha-‘hīnai’c‍cādi, taināti anunā, kathaṃ taina yuttattaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhassāti hīnuk‍kaṭṭhasambandhamukhaina vibhāvaintau’tatthā’tiādimāha, tatthāti hīnaic‍cādinā vuttai tasmiṃ, yatauti uk‍kaṭṭhasāriputtādikau, hīnauti pañ‍ñavattādi(tau nik‍kaṭṭhau, sau sāriputtādikau uk‍kaṭṭhau) hīnatthavisayau jāyatai… tabbisayai tassa uk‍kaṭṭhatā pattitau, hīnuk‍kaṭṭhasambandhau pana atisāyanādikāya kāyaci kiriyāya katauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

14. satta

kintaṃ ādhikyamic‍cāha- ‘adhikādhikīsambandhau’ti, adhikau assa atthīti adhikī=khārī, athādhikīvinimuttai adhikādhikisambandhau kathamavasīyatai nahyatrādhikī kauci sūyatic‍cāha-‘navinai’c‍cādi, iti saddau haitumhi yathāvuttasambandhasādhanau, tadaupasaddainayaugauti tadā upasaddainādhikinauyaugauti atthau| dviṭṭhattā sambandhassa adhikassāpyupasaddaina yaugā tataupi kasmā na sattamītyāha-‘adhikamhātvi’c‍cādi, yathā aikāyaiva vibhattiyā ubhayagatassāpi sambandhassa jautitattā vij‍jusaṅkhātalakkhiyā sattamyabhāvau, aivamidhā pīti pubbai vuttasamādhimatidisantau āha-‘lakkhitā viya sattamya bhāvau’ti, ‘‘māṇikā caturaudauṇā, khāritthī catumāṇikā’’|

15. sāmi

kintaṃ  sāmittamic‍cāha-‘sassāmisambandhau’ti, kāya kiriyāya janitauti āha-‘paripālanādikiriyāya janitau’ti, tañ‍cāti sāmittāpaikkhau napuṃsakaniddaisau, sambandhassānaikavidhattā āha‘sabbattha sambandhai’ti, ākhyāyataitīmassa atthau vidhīyatai ñāpīyataiti, diṭṭhantau-panītautthau sukhaina paṭipattuṃ sak‍kāti pasiddhamanuvadiyamānamappasiddhañ‍ca vidhīyamānaṃ diṭṭhantamāha-‘yathā yau kuṇḍalī sau daivadattau’ti, kuṇḍalittampasiddhaṃ, daivadattattamappasiddhamic‍cāha-‘kuṇḍalittānuvādainai’c‍cādi| yathaivaubhayādhiṭṭhānattaipyupasaj‍janatauva chaṭṭhī ‘rañ‍ñau purisau’ti, na visaissatau ‘purisassa rājā’ti, tathā sambandhābhibyañ‍janakainādhinā yaugaipi visaisanatauva bhavati, nāñ‍ñatauti āha-‘tatthā’tiādi| byatiraikamāpādīyatīti kārakachak‍katau byatirittattā sambandhau byatiraikau taṃ āpādīyati pāpīyatīti atthau, ubhayatauti saṃtau sāmitau ca, adhibrahmadattai pañ‍cālāti brahmadattassa pañ‍cālā santakā sāminau (pautthakai) ti atthau, santakā sāminau (pautthakai) hi pañ‍cālā, na sāmi nasaṃ (pautthakai), adhipañ‍cālaisu brahmadattauti pañ‍cālānaṃ brahmadattau sāmītyatthau|

16. kattu

kiṃ lakkhaṇau-yaṃ kattāic‍cāha-‘kattari’c‍cādi, gacchati daivadattauc‍cādau kattari patiṭṭhitaṃ, pacatyaudanaṃ daivadattauc‍cādau kammai patiṭṭhitaṃ kiriyaṃ karautīti sambandhau, karautīti ca iminā anvattha byapadaisauva siddhau-yaṃ kattu vauhārauti ñāpaiti, kainaci payuj‍jamānaupi sakai kammai sayamaiva padhānattamanubhavatīti‘payuttau vā padhānabhāvainā’ti vuttaṃ hauti caittha-

padhānatāya yau kattu, kammaṭṭhaṃ kurutai kriyaṃ|

sā kattā nāmappayuttau, payuttau vātyayaṃ dvidhāti||

tattha purisaina katanti appayuttau, purisaina karauti daivadattauti payuttau, nanu kiriyā-yamabyavadhānaina karaṇādhikaraṇai haiva  sādhyatai kattārā tu dūraṭṭhaina, tathāsati kathamanaikasādhanasādhanīyaṃ kiriyaṃ kattāva karauti mukhyabhāvaināparaitvamukhyabhāvaināti vuc‍catai-

kattutauñ‍ñaisamubbhūtyā, vivittālaucanādinā vavivaikaica dassanāti vākyapadīyai, pavivaikaica kārakantharāmabhāvaidissabhai kattarikriyā atthi bhavati vij‍jatic‍cādau titaṭīkā, tatiyapāthānaṃpāta 100, naṃpāta 243|

dūrādapyupakārittai, kattu vāttappadhānatā||

ayamaittha atthau ‘‘kattunau sakāsā añ‍ñaisaṃ karaṇādīnaṃ ubbhūtyā sambhavainaca, tathāhi pasiddhakaraṇasabhāvaupi pharasu kattāyattavuttitāyādhikaraṇambha vati‘pharasumhi ṭhapaitvā chindatī’ti, vivittassa karaṇādīhi puthabbhūtassa, kattunau kiriyāya ālaucanaina dassanaina ca ‘daivadattau atthi bhavati vij‍jatī’ti vuthabbhūtassānaina ālaucanaina dassanainaca kattunaudaivadattainadaivadattaṃ bhavati vij‍jatīti (pautthakai), ādisaddaina karaṇādinau (tadadhīna) pavattiādīnaṃ gahaṇaṃ, iminā kāraṇaina dūratau upakārittaipi kattuyaiva sappadhānatā siyā’’ti, punapi-

thālyādīnantu kattuttaṃ, hautaivācaitanānapi|

yatau saddassa vutyatthai, micchātyāsaina-nādinā||

ayamaittha atthau ‘‘acaitanānampi thālīasiādīnaṃ kattuttambhavati yathāvaṇṇitasappadhānattanimittābhāvaipi, kasmāti cai yatau kāraṇā atthai attanau abhidhaiyyaisaddassa vutti paṭipatti anādinā micchābhyāsainabhavati tasmā thālyādīnantiādi pakataṃ, kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti ‘‘añ‍ñathā saddatthau, añ‍ñathā sabhāvatthau, na hi saddau bāhyai vatthumhi byāpāramāpaj‍jati, tathāhi–

añ‍ñathai vāggi saṃsaggā, dāhaṃ daḍḍhauti mañ‍ñati|

añ‍ñathā dāhasaddaina, dāhatthau sampatīyatai||

bāhyapadatthassa sabhāvau viya hutvā micchābhyāsaina anādinā pana pavattaina bāhyai vatthūni avij‍jamānaina karaṇabhūtaina thālyādīnaṃ kattuttaṃ hautī’’ti| atha ca pana–

vatticchādau  pavattaihi, sappadhānatta haituhi|

kattudhammaihi vuttaihi, kattā saddāva ñāyatai||

aikassāmukhyavatthāhi, bhaidaina parikappanai|

kammattaṃ karaṇattañ‍ca, kattuttaṃ caupajāyatai||

vuttaihīti ‘kattutauñ‍ñaisamubbhūtyā’c‍cādinā, saddāvāti saddai taidhammā hauntu vā mā vā kattusambandhiyathāvuttadhammavacanicchāya saddatauva kattā patīyatai, niyamaina na vatthutauti atthau, amukhyā vatthāhīti buddhiyā tadākāratāya pariṇāmāvatthāhi, hantyatta nāvattānanti aikassaivāttanau kammattādi jāyatai, atauyaivāc‍cantamasantaupi aṅkurau jananakiriyāya kattā bhavati ‘aṅkurau jāyatai’ti, vuttaṃ hi–

purāsañ‍janitau purāsañ‍jātitau bhāvā, buddhayavatthāni bandhanau|

avasiṭṭhau satāñ‍ñaina, kattā bhavati jātiyāiti||

satā añ‍ñaina daivadattādinā|

kintaṃ karaṇaṃ nāmāti āha-‘yau panā’tiādi, yauti yau padatthau, iminā idaṃ dīpaiti ‘‘karaṇabyapadaisāya suttantarābhāvaipi karautyanainaiti karaṇantyanvatthatau pasiddhau-yaṃ karaṇavauhārau’’ti, atraidaṃ paṭibhānaṃ–

yaṃ vattumicchitaṃ kattu, kriyāyac‍cantasādhanaṃ|

karaṇantaṃ dvidhā hauti, bāhyabbhantarabhaidatauti||

tattha yaṃvattumicchitaṃtyādinā idaṃ dassaiti-‘‘na vatthutau kiñ‍ci karaṇaṃ nāma atthi, vacanicchāvasaina pana pasiddhakaraṇānampi pharasuādīnaṃ ‘pharasu chindati, pharasumhi chindatī’ti kattuadhikaraṇattassapi, pasiddhādhikaraṇānampi tadanuttādivisaisasambhavai thālyā pacatī’ti karaṇattassapi dassanatau vacanicchātauyaiva karaṇaṃ kārakaṃ viñ‍ñaiyya’’nti, tañ‍ca duvidhanti āha-‘dvidhā hautī’tiādi, tattha asinā chindatīti bāhyaṃ, cakkhunā passatītyabbhantaraṃ| kiriyānimittattā kārakanti iminā kārakasaddassa nimittapariyāyattamāha| pakati yābhirūpauc‍cādau  kiriyāyāvij‍jamānattā sambandhamattaṃ viñ‍ñāyatīti sambandhalakkhaṇā chaṭṭhī siyā, samaina dhāvatītyādau tu kiriyāya vij‍jamānattaipi kammattaṃ vattumicchitanti dutiyā siyā, samaṃ dhāvati visamaṃ dhāvatītyatthau, tathā dvidauṇaināti dvaidauṇai kiṇātītyatthau, pañ‍cakaināti pañ‍ca parimāṇassa pañ‍cakau pañ‍cakaṃ vaggaṃ katvā pasavau kiṇāti pañ‍capañ‍ca katvā kiṇātītyatthauti vāttikakāraina ‘tatiyā vidhānāyaupasaṅkhyānaṃ’kataṃ, taṃ dassaitumāha- ‘pakatiādīhī’tiādi, yatthaisaṃkaraṇabhāvauti yassaṃ kiriyāyamai sampakatyādīnaṃ karaṇattaṃ, taṃ kiriyaṃ kathayatīti sambandhau, sabbassaiva padatthassa sattābyabhicāratau āha-‘bhū’ic‍cādi, pakatiyābhirūpauti sabhāvaināyamabhirūpau bhavati, na tu vatthālaṅkārādinaityatthau, karautissa gammamānattai pana pakatiyābhirūpau katautiādi, tadā kattariyaiva tatiyā, yaṃ yasmā añ‍ñagauttau gautamau na hauti atauca gautamau bhavatīti sambandhau, nanuca dvidauṇa pañ‍cakā kayampaṭic‍ca kammabhūtā haunti dvidauṇādinā kaiyattā tatau kimuc‍catai‘kayampaṭic‍ca dvidauṇapañ‍cakā karaṇāni bhavantī’tyā saṅkīyāha-‘tathāhi’c‍cādi, idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti-‘‘dvidauṇādisaddaina hi na gayhupagaṃ vatthumuc‍cati, taihi pana dvidauṇādyatthaṃ mūlaṃ tādatthiyā bhihitanti na vuttadausāvasarau’’ti|

17. saha

yathāk‍kamamāgamanakiriyāya thūlattaguṇaina gaumantadabbaina ca sambandhaṃ dassaituṃ ‘taṃ yathai’c‍cādimāha, udāharaṇamattaṃ dassaintaupi iminā adhippāyaināpi dassaitīti vuttaṃ ‘sabbatthaiva vā’tiādi, puttaina sahāgatautiādīsu āgamanādikiriyampaṭic‍ca dvai kattārau padhānāppadhānavasaina, taisu padhānai kāriyasampac‍cayañāyaina vā bahulaṃvacanaina vā padhānai kattari pac‍cayau, itarai’kattari tatiyā’tyaiva tatiyā siddhā, tatau-yaṃ vacanārambhau-natthakauti mañ‍ñamānaucau daiti ‘nanvi’c‍cādinā, ttainābhidhīyatai pitaiti padhānakattā, tulyaupi kiriyāsambandhai pitāputtānaṃ pitātra padhānaṃ saddacauditattā, puttau-ppadhānamasaddacauditattā, saddabyāpārāpaikkhāya hi padhānāppadhānāya vavatthānaṃ, pituraivātrāgatādisaddasāmānādhikaraṇyā āgamanakiriyā sambandhau saddainauc‍catai, naitarassa saddasattisabhāvatau, tathā vidhā hi saddassa satti yatau pituraivātra kiriyā sambandhaṃ sak‍kauti paṭipādaituṃ, na puttassa, aivañ‍cāgatauc‍cādau aikavacanamupaj‍jatai, añ‍ñathā hi bahuvacanaṃ siyā, ‘pitā puttā āgatā’ti| (yadi) vuttanayaina tatiyā siddhā ‘siddhai satyārambhau niyamāya vā vikappāya vā’ na caittha niyamau, nāpi vikappau, tatau kimanaina yaugainā tyāsayaināha-‘appadhānaiyaiva’c‍cādi| nayidamaivamic‍cādi parihārau| sahabhāvamattaṃ vattumiṭṭhaṃ vāti yaujanā, sāmatthiyāti bhaidādhiṭṭhānattā sahatthayaugassa puttassāpi kiriyābhisambandhau viñ‍ñāyati kathamañ‍ñathā sahatthayaugau siyāti iminā attha balaina| atthaggahaṇanti suttai ‘‘sahatthainai’’ti atthaggahaṇaṃ| pariyāyatthanti paritau ayanti avagacchanti atthamanainaiti pariyāyau atthau payaujanaṃ yassāti viggahau, aittha ca pariyāyatthamaivāti nāvadhāraṇaṃ, atauyaiva vināpi sahasaddappayaugaṃ tappariyāyappayaugaṃ vā sahatthaina yaugai vidhānatau asatyapi sahādisaddappayaugai yattha tadatthau gamyatai tatthāpi bhavatyaiva tatiyā… gamyamānaina sahatthaina yaugā, yathā‘‘syādi syānai’’ti| (3-1) saha pariyāyaināti sahasaddassa pariyāyatau| visaisānupādānatauti ‘‘sahayuttai-ppadhānai’’ti (2-3-19) pāṇinīya suttai viya appadhānaiti visaisassānupādānatau|

18. lakkhaṇai

lakkhīyatyanaināti lakkhaṇaṃ saṅkaitau, kintaṃ kamaṇḍaluppabhuti, atthai kāriyassāsambhavā taṃvācīhi vibhattīti mañ‍ñamānaina yaṃvivaraṇaṃ kataṃ ‘lakkhaṇai vattamānatau’ti, tamul‍laṅgiyātthaṃ vadati ‘yau atthau’ic‍cādi, vattamānatauti viñ‍ñāyati… nāmasmā pac‍cayavidhānappa saṅgatau, upāttauti gahitau| kañ‍cippaṇaramāpan‍nassa lakkhaṇai tatiyā yathā siyā ‘rukkhampati vij‍jautana’mic‍catra lakkhaṇamattai mā  bhavīti pāṇinīyaihi ‘‘itthambhūtalakkhaṇai’’ti (2-3-21) itthambhūtaggahaṇaṃ kataṃ, tammanasi nidhāya caudayamāha–‘nanvi’c‍cādi, itthambhūtassa lakkhaṇanti chaṭṭhīsamāsau, āpan‍nassāti bhūtassa, sāmañ‍ñassa bhaidakau visaisau pakārau vuc‍catai, tathāhi ‘tidaṇḍakaina paribbājakamaddakkhī’ti manussattasāmañ‍ñassa paribbājakattampakārau, tamparibbājakau pattau, tassa tidaṇḍakaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ| upalakkhaṇattaiti iminā itthambhūtassa lakkhaṇattābhāvamāha, upalakkhaṇanti saṅkaitau| nayidamatthic‍cādi parihārau, rukkhampati vij‍jautananti aittha kassaci itthambhūtassābhāvā itthambhūtaggahaṇai payaujanaṃ na dissati tan‍nivattanatau, rukkhampati vij‍jautataiti aitthatu itthambhūtassa vij‍jusaṅkhātassa atthassa sabbhāvā tatiyāppattiyaṃ taṃnivattiyā yathāvuttaṃ ñāyamantaraina nāñ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci paṭisaraṇamatthīti sañ‍ñāpayamāha-‘avassa’mic‍cādi, añ‍ñathāti aivamanabbhupagamma ‘itthambhūtalakkhaṇai’ti vacanai karīyamānai sati, itthambhūtavādinau uttaramāsaṅkiyāha-‘nacā’tiādi, vattunti vacanasaisau, cau-vadhāraṇai, naiva sak‍kāti vuttaṃ hauti, dutiyāvidhānasāmatthiyāti ‘‘patiparīhi bhāgai cā’’ti (2-9) kasmā na sak‍kāti āha-‘atthi’c‍cādi, asati hi payaujanai sāmatthiyanti bhāvau| atha kamaṇḍalu pāṇimhi assāti ādau tatiyā kasmā na siyā tyāha-‘kamaṇḍalu’ic‍cādi, kamaṇḍalussa saitassa ca upalakkhaṇa bhāvainānupādānatauti sambandhau, iminā upādātunau savacasi satantatamāha, visiṭṭhāvayavādhaiyyaināti pāṇi kamaṇḍalunā visiṭṭhauvayavau, tassādhaiyyau kamaṇḍalu taināti atthau| yaināṅgaina vikataina aṅginau vikārau lakkhīyatai tatau tatiyā yathāsiyāti paraihi ‘‘yaināṅgavikārau’’ti (2-3-20) āraddhaṃ, tadāha-‘yainai’c‍cādi, tattha yasaddaina vikatāvayavau haituttaina niddiṭṭhau aṅgasaddau ca samudāyavacanau upāttau taināha-‘yainaic‍cādi, nanuca kāṇādīnaṃ niyataguṇīnamabhidhāyittā akkhisaddassa payaugau na yuttau byāvattiyābhāvāti, naisa dausau, laukiyau-yaṃ saddassa vauhārau, laukassa ca saddappayaugai gurulaghumpatyadayau natthi, tathāhi laukaina ‘sīsapāti’pi vuc‍catai, rukkhau  sīsapātipi, yassa tu tatthādarau, sau-kkhisaddaṃ payuj‍jataiva, athavā sāmañ‍ñupak‍kamai visaisau payaugamarahati, akkhi(nāti) hi vuttai sandaihau siyā ‘kimassa vattumicchita’nti tatau kāṇauti vuc‍catai, nākkhinā nirūpayati saubhanauti, kiñ‍carahi kāṇauti, akkhikāṇamassaityādau kasmā na siyāti āsaṅkiya‘kamaṇḍalu pāṇimhi assā’tyatra yau vuttau parihārau, ta maitthaupadisantau āha-‘haiṭṭhā viya appasaṅgau’ti|

19. haitu

kismiñ‍ci phalai diṭṭhasāmatthiyaitthai haitu niruḷhau| aikatra hi diṭṭhasāmatthiyautthau taṃyauggatāya kāriya (ma)karauntaupi haitu vuc‍catai sati saha kārinyasati ca vidhurappac‍cayau papā(ta) tau, taina kāriyassa byabhicārau, tainaivāha- ‘anipphādayantaupī’ti, kāraṇai tatiyāyābhāvai kāraṇamāha- ‘tak‍kiriyāyauggatāmattai’c‍cādi|

21. guṇai

upādhi visaisanaṃ, parāṅgabhūtaiti parassa bandhattā(dinau) vidhānā yāṅgabhūtai jaḷattādihaitumhīti atthau, iminā guṇasaddau yamappadhānavacanauti vadati, yañ‍cāppadhānaṃ tamparāṅgabhūtanti guṇaitīmassa ‘parāṅgabhūtai’ti atthamāha, yañhi paratthamupādīyatai tadappadhānamanu vādarūpainaupādīyamānattā, yathā ‘jaḷattā baddhau, rukkhau yaṃ sīsa pattā, aggi aittha dhumattai’c‍cādi, tainaivāha-‘parattharūpāpan‍na’nti, vāvidhānanti ‘‘vibhāsā guṇai-thiyaṃ’’nti (2-3-25) aivaṃ vidhānaṃ, itthiyantiādinā pañ‍camīrūpassapi abhimatabhāvaṃ dassaiti, tainaivāha-‘ubhayatthā’tiādi, nanu ‘tau pañ‍camyā’’ti (4-95) suttaina taddhitapac‍cayantassa kathaṃ pañ‍camyantatā tyāsaṅkiyāha-‘pañ‍caminiddaisai’ ic‍cādi, iminā suttaina pañ‍camiyā vināti yaujanā, vā pañ‍camiyā vidhānatau tatiyārūpainapi bhavitabbanti caudanamanasi nidhāya pañ‍camyu dāharaṇaiyaiva kāraṇaṃ tatiyaudāharaṇassa ca paṭhamaudāharaṇānusārainānu mantabbatañ‍cadassaintau āha ‘pañ‍camyāyaivi’c‍cādi, abhāvaina saṅkhāraniraudhaināti tatiyantarūpāni|

22. chaṭṭhī

sambandhavacanicchāyaivaittha  chaṭṭhī siddhāti āsaṅkiyāha-‘haitumhi’c‍cādi, haitumāha-‘taṃ yauggatā’ ic‍cādi, saddasattisāmatthiyāti saddasattisaṅkhātāya sāmatthiyā, tamaiva phuṭayati ‘yattha’ic‍cādinā, haitvatthavācakaihi antaraināti sambandhau, samānādhikaraṇaihīti haitvatthasamānādhikaraṇaihi udarādīhi, taj‍jautanāyāti haitvatthajautanāyālanti sambandhau, byatiraikaina tu haitvatthatau tappavattimāha-‘yattha pani’c‍cādi, haitvatthaihipic‍cādinā sāmatthiyamupadassitaṃ ‘vasai dhammassa haitunau’ti tatthaudāharaṇaṃ|

23. sabbā

nāmabhūtaihīti sabbādīsu kaici kaisañ‍ci yadi nāmāni siyuṃ taihi, appadhānabhūtaihīti piyasabbādīhi, kimpayaujanantiādau payaujanasaddau ‘‘payaujanaṃ kāriyahaitūsu’’ti nighaṇḍutau haituvacanau|

24. catu

yassa sacaitanāyācaitanāya vā kassaci, pūjānuggahakāmatta mantaraina sammāpadānaṃ na sambhavatīti āha-‘pūjā’ ic‍cādi, taināti yathāvuttainatthaina, cāgaṅganti dadātinau dānakiriyāya kammaina sambajjhamānañ‍cāgakāraṇaṃ, idāni sampadānalakkhaṇaṃ sappabhaidamāvikātukāmau āha-‘tadida’mic‍cādi, nanirākarautītya nirākaraṇaṃ, ārādhayatītyārādhanaṃ, abbhanujānātītyabbhanuñ‍ñaṃ, taisaṃ vasaina, idha sampadānantidhā vuttaṃ kathitaṃ, kaitaiti āha-‘rukkhayācakabhikkhavau’ti, rukkhādikaṃ taṃ sampadānaṃ vibhāgaina dassaitumāha-‘tattha hi’c‍cādi, ajjhaisanainārādhanaina sampadānaṃ bhavatīti sambandhau, anumatiyā abbhanujānaina, vij‍jamānaipyanirākaraṇai bhikkhunau-dhikamanumatantyadhippāyaināha-‘sau’ ic‍cādi|

25. tāda

payaujanapariyāyai atthasaddai sati idanti payaujanaṃ parāmaṭṭhuṃ sak‍kāti ‘tassaida’ntiādi vuttaṃ, tasaddainaittha vikatiyātisambandhau, atthasaddaina  tu pakatiyā, attha kathanamaitanti iminā vacanicchāya maitissaṃ natthi samāsauti dīpaiti, viggahai vāsmimimasmāyaiva nipātanā samāsau daṭṭhabbau,sau vikatisaṅkhātau atthau, pakatitvañ‍ñapadatthainākkhipīyatai, tatau tadatthasaddatau, kauyaṃ tadattha bhāvau c‍cāha-‘tadatthabhāvau nāmā’tiādi, nimittaṃ yūpādivikati, nimittīdāruādipakati tasaddainātthasaddaina ca gahitā, taisaṃ sambandhau tadatthasaddassa pavattinimittaṃ tadatthabhāvau nāmāti atthau, haitu haitumantabhāvalakkhaṇau sambandhau nāmāti vuttaṃ hauti, padhānāvacchaidaguṇasmāti vidhīyamānattaina padhānassa purisassa ācchaidakā visaisakā guṇā, yañhi vidhīyatai ñāpīyatai tampadhāna, mitaramappadhānamanuvaj‍jamānattā, yadā upādīyataiti vatvā upādīyamānappakāraṃ diṭṭhantaina phuṭayitumāha-‘yathā’ic‍cādi, yūpau nimittaṃ yassa dārunau taṃ tathā vuttaṃ nāñ‍ñanimittanti yadā nimityavacchaidakattainupādīyatai tadā nimittasaddatauva catutthī sambandhau, tatau añ‍ñanimittatau, nimittanimittīnaṃ sambandhassa abhimukhabhāvaina tatthaiva nimittai pavattīti sambandhau, nimittasaddatau haitutatiyāpi nāsaṅkanīyāti sambandhau, kāraṇamāha-‘nimitta’ haitu ic‍cādi| ubhayatthāti yūpāya pākāyāti ubhayattha|

piṇiyamānā kiṃ saddādivacanīyā parau sāduādikau yau-tthau sau añ‍ñau kattā yassa sau añ‍ñakattukau-bhilāsau, abhilāsau rucīti iminā dityādyanaikatthantaupi rucisaddau-bhilāsai ruḷhauva gayhatīti vadati, sā ruci atthau-bhidhaiyyau yaisaṃ bhai tadatthā, taisaṃ sambandhinau piṇiyamānassa, iminā ca kiñ‍cāpi rucirayaṃ yassuppaj‍jataiyau vā tassā janakau, taisamubhin‍nampi atthi, tathāpi yassuppaj‍jatai sauva tassādhikaraṇaṃ, ajanakaupi sau tāya saṃsattauti tassaiva piṇiyamānassa sampadānasañ‍ñā, natujanakassa tāyāsaṃsattassāti dīpaiti, idhāti iminā sak‍katai udāharaṇānamasamānarūpakaṃ dassaiti, iminā ca udāharaṇānamasamāna rūpattamaiva  catutthīvidhānai kāraṇaṃ, añ‍ñathā chaṭṭhīyaiva taihipi vidhīyaiyya kāraṇāsambhavā kāraṇasabbhāvā hi dīpaiti, īdisassa pac‍cudāharaṇa(ssa) bhāvai kāraṇamāha-‘anañ‍ñakattu kattā-bhilāsassā’ti, paraisamidampac‍cudāharaṇambhavatu idha kathanti āha-‘ihatvi’c‍cādi, tvanti attamattai paṭhamāyaiva, sambhavai kāraṇantaramāha-‘athavā’tiādi, natthi añ‍ñau kattā yassā sā anañ‍ñakattukā ruci, bhāya ruciyā sambandhinau piṇiyamānassa sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ yai pana icchantīti sambandhau, yai pana āhu cātisambandhau, uppaj‍janamuppatti, kassa uppatti, ruciyā, uppattiyā ādhārau uppatyādhārau‘daivadattāya raucatai’c‍cādau daivadattādiṃ upalakkhaitīti uppatyādhāraupalakkhaṇaṃ vacanaṃ, aittha ruciyā uppatyādhāraupalakkhaṇamaiva vacananti sāvadhāraṇa matthau daṭṭhabbau… sabbaisaṃ vākyānamavadhāraṇaphalattā, taina piṇiya mānavacanamabhilāsassa añ‍ñakattukatāsandassanaparaṃ na hautīti byavacchin‍nambhavati, taisaṃ kac‍cāyanassa caihāpi pasaj‍jatīti sambandhau, ihāpīti tvamiti aitthāpi, añ‍ñathāsiddhattāti atthamattai paṭhamāya aiva siddhattā, vacanassāti ubhin‍nampi catutthīvidhānavacanassa, atippasaṅgaucāti ‘‘kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisī’’tiādau tavāti anabhimataṭṭhānappattica|

dhārippayaugaiti dhāridhātunau payaugai, uttamīṇau adhamīṇauti dhanikagahaitūnaṃ ruḷhītyāha-‘uttamīṇau dhanasāmī’ti, uttamīṇamassāti uttamīṇau, dhārayatītimassatthamāha-‘vahatī’ti, dhāritauti dhāraṃdhātutau| suttantaraina (taṃ taṃ) niyamaina niyamitānaṃ taisaṃtaisaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ vidhāya catutthī vihitā paraihi, tadāha ‘silāgha hanuṭṭhāsapāna’mic‍cādi, sabbattha yaugasambandhai chaṭṭhī, ñāpaitunti silāghādikai baudhaituṃ, icchitauti atimatau| tadatthavācīnañ‍ca dhātūnaṃ saṅgahaṇatthaṃ ‘kudhaduhissausūyatthāna’nti vuttaṃ, yampati kaupauti iminā kaupassa visayamāha, yassāti iminā yaṃkārakanti vuttamaiva parāmasati, yassāti rājādinau, pucchatīti pucchiyatai, tainaiva vakkhati-‘yassa sambandhiṃ subhāsubhaṃ pucchati naimittikau’ti, yaucānukūlattambha jatīti iminā idaṃ dassaiti ‘‘patipubbakau āpubbakauca suṇauti abbhupagamai paṭijānanaica vattatai, yadāyamabbhupagamau patthanā pubbatau bhavati tadā patthanāya kattu patthayitunau catutthī rūpambhavati, yadātvapatthatampi kaivalamānukūlyaina vattamānaṃdisvā daiyyamabbhupagacchanti idamidaṃ vaudassāmāti kaupissasāmāti (pautthakai) tadānukūlyaina pavattanasaṅkhātāya kiriyāya kattunau catutthīrūpambhavatī’’ti, iminā ca pāṇiniyavuttikārassa jayādic‍cassamatai byabhicāramāha, sau hi ‘‘abbhupagamau nāma paraina patthitassa bhavati tattha patthayituyaiva catutthīrūpambhavatī’’ti vadati, pubbassākiriyāya yau kattāti vaidavākyaina vihitathutikakaraṇa saṅkhātāya ussāhānujānanatau pubbabhūtāya kiriyāya yau kattā hautā pautā ca, sabbatthāpi‘tassā’ti idaṃ ‘catutthīyāyadabhimataṃ rūpa’ntiiminā sambandhitabbaṃ, chaṭṭhiyā sabbabyāpibhāvamālamba aivanti atidaisauti āha ‘aivantī’tiādi, rūpasāmyatau pana tādatthiyatthasabbhāvāca yathāyaugaṃ tādatthya catutthīpi na virujjhati, vakkhati hi 'tādatthya vacanicchāyantu‘ ic‍cādinā, icchitau rājā, kamma vacanicchāti iminā vacanicchāya padhānattamāha… vacanicchābhimukhattā sabbaisaṃ kārakānaṃ, appacurappayaugattāti abahuppayaugattā, hanutai apanayati, upatiṭṭhatai upaṭṭhānaṃ upagamanaṃ karauti, pihayanti patthainti, kujjha amarisaṃ karauti, duhanādayau kaupappabhavā nānantaraina tai sambhavantīti dūbhādayaupyudāhaṭā ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ti (1-33) rassai aparajjhāmi, issayanti nasahanti, ārajjhati pucchitau naimittikau upaparikkhati, bhādatthhaṃ vibhāvaiti ‘yassai’c‍cādinā, pariyā laucaiti upaparikkhati, kiṃ lakkhaṇau-yamaniyamauc‍cāha-‘nāvassa’mic‍cādi, yācitusmāyaiva yācituyaiva (pautthakai) chaṭṭhīti nāvassamayan‍niyamauti atthau, kutauc‍cāha-‘sambandhai’c‍cādi, añ‍ñasmāpīti ayācitutauapi, thutikaraṇassa kattubhūtā hautāpautārau ussāhanānujānanānaṃ kammaṃ hautīti āha-‘pac‍canupubbassa giṇātissa kammai’ti| hautupautusaddaihic‍cādinā  pautusaddassapi chaṭṭhirūpaṃ dassitaṃ, vuttiyantu ‘hautu patigiṇāti’c‍caiva pāṭhau dissati, hautvaivampityamhākamatraupaikkhā, ussāhayatītyatthai giṇāti antaugataṇyatthau| anāvādauti nāvāan‍nādivaj‍jitai| aittha pana tathā paṭisaidhābhāvaipi bahulādhikāratau ‘na taṃ nāvammañ‍ñai nataṃ an‍nammañ‍ñai’c‍cādi bhavati, atthaiva hi bahulādhikāratauvā tippasaṅgābhāvau, paṭhamantaṃ rūpanti mañ‍ñamānauti paṭhamantaṃ rūpaṃ, aitthaca yadā tiṇāditaupi khikaṃsīyatai tadā nindāvagamau, tattha ca niyaugatauva nañpayaugau ñāthānupa pattiyāti sappaṭisaidhamudāhaṭaṃ ‘na mañ‍ñamānau’ti, avadhimhiyaiva pañ‍camītiṇāti, nañpayaugaiva niyaugatau nindāvagamā tiṇanti kammaiyaiva dutiyā, athavā satīpi nindāvagamai vacanicchātau savisayaiyaiva dutiyā, sammutippayaugaiti sammutisaddassa payaugai, catutthiṃ icchantīti mattāyāti aittha catutthiṃ icchanti, puna ruttattai kāraṇamāha-‘atha sautivij‍jamānattā’ti, caṇḍassa kuk‍kurassātiādisuttai ‘‘atha sau’’ti pāṭhassa vij‍jamānattā ‘bhiyyausau mattāyā’ti aittha saukārau punaruttauti adhippāyau, caggahaṇaināti ‘‘silāghahanu…pai॰… sattamyatthaisucā’’ti suttai caggahaṇaina, catutthībhimatāti ‘‘namau yaugādisvapicā’’ti suttaina|

26. pañ‍ca

avadhīyatai visilissatai aitasmātyavadhi, avapubbāya dhādhātutau avadhimhi‘‘dādhātvī’’ti (5-45) i|

yatau-vadhīyatai kiñ‍ci, kriyāpubbaṃ kutaucipi|

calañ‍cācalamañ‍ñañ‍ca, taṃ vadantya vadhimbudhā||

aitthāyamatthau ‘‘yataukutaucipi atthau kiñ‍ci vatthuavadhīyatai saṃyaugassa vā kiriyānimittaṃ gamanadānādikiriyāpubbaṃ visilissatai sayaṃ vā apaiti añ‍ñaina vā visuṃ karīyatai, tamaivaṃvidhaṃ calañ‍cā calañ‍ca visilaisaparicchaidabhūtaṃ padatthamavadhiṃ vadanti budhā tabbidunau’’ti, taṃ yathā dhāvatāssā purisau patati, purisaṃ pātaiti vā, rukkhā paṇṇaṃ  patati, paṇṇaṃ pātaiti vāti, sauca tidhāpyuc‍catai visayabhaidaina, yathā–

niddiṭṭhavisayau kauci, upāttavisayau tathā|

anumaiyyavisayau cāti, tidhāvadhi samīritauti||

tattha niddiṭṭhā vacanaupanītā kiriyā visayau yattha sau niddiṭṭha visayau vadhi, yathā-‘gāmasmā āgatau’ti, upāttājjhāhaṭā kiriyā visayau yattha sau upāttavisayau, yathā-‘valāhakā vij‍jautatai’ti, valāhakā nikkhamma vij‍jaubhataiti atthau, anumaiyyā kiriyā visayau yattha sau anumaiyyavisayau, yathā‘māthurā pāṭaliputtakaihi abhirūpatarā’ti, aittha abhirūpaguṇaina uk‍kaṃsanamanumaiyyaṃ, atauyaivaitthāyamaiva sañ‍ñā paripphuṭā viñ‍ñāyatīti na kācidiha sañ‍ñā paribhāsīyatai byāmauhakāriṇī, athāvadhismāti vuc‍camānai ‘padatthāvadhismā’ti kutau-vasīyatai yainaivaṃ vivaraṇaṃ katantyāsaṅkiyāha-padāna’mic‍cādi| yathā gāmasmātiādinā idaṃ dīpaiti ‘‘na kaivalaṃ kāyasaṃsaggapubbakauva visilaisau, kiñ‍carahi buddhisaṃsaggapubbakaupi, atthi caiha buddhisaṃsagga pubbakau, tathāhi cauraihi bhāyatityādau yau (bhaya)paikkhaṇa sīlau bhavati, sau yadi maṃ caurā passaiyyuṃ, dhuvaṃ mai maraṇanti vicāraintau tai buddhiyā pappauti pappuyya ca tatau nivattatai, tathā cauraihi tāyatityādau yau aikassa mittau bhavati sau yadyaitaṃ caurā passaiyyuṃ dhuvamassa maraṇanti passantau tai buddhiyā patvā tatau nivattayatītyavadhittā avadhismāva sabbattha pañ‍camī’’ti, paraihi pana aitādisaisu sabbattha paṭipattuvisaisānuggahāya suttaihaiva papañ‍cau katau, idha pana sāmañ‍ñavacanainaiva siddhattā tadanuggahāya udāharaṇaihaiva papañ‍citaṃ, bhītibhāyanatthānanti bhīti tāyanānaṃ atthau yaisaṃ tai bhītitāyanatthā taisanti atthau, nanu bhītyatthānaṃ payaugai bhayahaitu sambhavati natu tāyanatthānanti, naisa dausau, tāyanañhi rakkhanamuc‍catai sāyanāhi rakkhanamuppaj‍jabhai (pautthakai) tañ‍ca bhayahaitutau vaityāha-‘yau  bhayahaitu’tiādi, iminā ca ‘‘bhītyatthānaṃ bhayahaitu’’ti (1-4-25) pāṇiniyānamapādānasañ‍ñā suttampaṭikkhipati, tatauti bhayahaitutau, taisanti bhayatāyanānaṃ, bhayamaniṭṭhapātasaṅkā, uttāsautyatthau, tāyana maniṭṭhapātatau parirakkhanaṃ, yathā dassanaṃ hauntaivātīminā vacanicchāyaiva padhānattamāha, kau hi laukiyaṃ vacanicchamativattituṃ sak‍kauti, tathāhi sabbaiva dabbādayau sabbakārakasattiyuttāva, tatthāyaṃ kārakavibhāgau vacanicchāvasainaiva, taṃ yathā ‘valāhakau vij‍jautatai, valāhakaina vij‍jautatai, valāhakai vij‍jautatai’ti| parāpubbassātiādinā ‘‘parājissāsayhau’’ti (1-4-26) sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, asayhau atthauti sahituṃ abhibhavitumasamatthau atthau, ajjhainasakāsāic‍cādinā udāharaṇatthamācikkhati, nanucaivamatthaṃ vadatā yañ‍ñadattassāsayhattaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ kathaṃ ajjhayanassāsayhattaṃ, yaināsayhattassāvadhittaṃ paṭipādayati tyāsaṅkiyāha-‘yañ‍ñadattānabhibhavau’c‍cādi, asayhautīmassa pac‍cudāharaṇaṃ paṭipādayati ‘yau pani’c‍cādi| ‘‘vāraṇatthānamicchitau’’ti (1-4-27) suttitaṃ, tadāha ‘vāraṇatthāna’mic‍cādi, vāraṇamatthau yaisaṃ tai vāraṇatthā, avadhimattavacanicchāyanti visaisatau vidhānamantarainā vadhimatta vacanicchāyaṃ, visaisavidhānaiti icchitauti visaisavidhānai, cittassāvadhivacanicchāyampana cittatau pāpaṃ nivārayaiti bhavati| ‘‘rakkhanatthānamicchita’’nti (2-6-3) suttitaṃ, tadāha-‘rakkhanatthāna’mic‍cādi, kaisañ‍cīti kac‍cāyanādikai dassaiti| ‘‘antaradhānai yainādassanamicchatī’’ti (1-4-28) sañ‍ñāsuttamāraddhaṃ, tadāha-‘antaradhānai’c‍cādi, ayamupajjhāyau yadi maṃ passatīti sambandhau, dassanai dausaṃ tak‍kaiti ‘nuna’mic‍cādinā, paisanamasak‍kārapubbakau niyaugau, ajjhaisanaṃ sak‍kārapubbakau, mañ‍ñamānau sissā ahaṃ kathan‍nāma byavahitau bhavāmīti antaradhānai byavadhāna nimittaṃ, yaina attanau adassanamicchatīti sambandhau, antaradhānaiti nimittasattamīcāyaṃ, atau āha-‘byavadhānanimitta’nti, byavahitauti antarahitau, māmaṃ upajjhāyautyādinā udāharaṇatthamāha, byavadhānatthaṃ byavahitatthaṃ, ‘‘antaradhānaiti kiṃ caurai na didikkhatī’’ti pac‍cudāhaṭaṃ  paraihi, tampaṭipādayamāha-‘yatthādassana’mic‍cādi, caurai na didikkhatī (tya), tra hi yau caurai na didikkhati taihi attanau adassanamicchatīti na antaradhānanimittaṃ, kintupaghātanivatyatthaṃ, kintupaghātanivattiyāti aivaṃ mañ‍ñatai ‘‘nātrāntaradhānamai (vāvasaiyaṃ), kintu tan‍nimittau paghātanivattipi, ‘upajjhāyā antaradhāyatī’tyatra vināpyupaghātasambhavamantaradhānasampādanamaiva sambhavatī’’ti| icchatiggahaṇaṃ kimatthaṃ adassanicchāyaṃ sati satyapi dassanai yathāsiyā’’ti vuttaṃ, (taṃ) paṭipādayamāha-‘adassanicchāyampani’c‍cādi, satīpi dassanaiti upajjhāyaina dassanai satīpi, tatauti upajjhāyatau, avadhirūpatā aivāti upajjhāyassāvadhirūpatā aiva| ‘‘ākhyātau payaugai’’ti (1-4-29) paravacanaṃ, tadāha ‘upayaugai’c‍cādi, niyamapubbakaṃ vij‍jāgahaṇamupayaugau trādhippaitau, upayaugavacanasāmatthiyā vij‍jāgahaṇatthaṃ sissavattaṃ niyamau, tatauti ākhyātutau| ‘‘janikattunau pakatī’’ti (1-4-30) sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ kataṃ, tadāha-‘janyatthassā’tiādi, janinau atthau janyatthau, kintaṃ jananaṃ, tatauti janikattuhaitutau, pakatisaddaina kāraṇamattaṃ gahitaṃparaihi ‘puttā pamaudā jāyatai’c‍cādau puttāditaupi yathā siyā’ti, duvidhaṃ (hi) kāraṇaṃ upādānakāraṇaṃ sahakārikāraṇañ‍ca, tattha kāriyainābhin‍nadaisau ghaṭassa mattikāpiṇḍau upādānakāraṇaṃ, tassaiva pana daṇḍacak‍kādi (kāriyaina bhin‍nadaisau) sahakārikāraṇaṃ, taisūpādānakāraṇassaiha gahaṇai’siṅgā sarau jāyatai’c‍catraiva siyā, na tu ‘puttā pamaudā jāyatai’c‍cādau, kārakantaravacanicchāyanti aittha kārakantarādivacanicchāyanti pāṭhaina bhavitabbaṃ… aitthantarai sambandha vacanicchāyapi sambhavatau, tatra tiṭṭhantīti tatraṭṭhā, taisaṃ, laukau-nañ‍ñathāvādī vauhārikajanau, tattha bhavā laukiyā, navattu paṭibaddhāti laukavacanicchamul‍laṅgiya ahamaiva vadāmīti sakajjhāsayaina vadantau vattā, tappaṭibaddhā na hautīti atthau| ‘‘pabhavau bhavatissā’’ti (1-4-31) suttitaṃ, tadāha-‘bhavatissā’tiādi, paṭhamatau upalabbhatīti atthakathanaina janyatthau-tra na sambhavatīti vadati, nahi himavā gaṅgāya kāraṇaṃ, sā hi añ‍ñaihi aivakāraṇaihi uppan‍nā, himavati tu kaivalaṃ paṭhamatau upalabbhati| ‘‘jigucchāvirāmapamādatthānumupasaṅkhyānaṃ’’ti (1-4-24vā) vuttaṃ, tadāha-‘viramaṇatthai’tyādi, jigucchāpamādatthadhātuyaugai pana kammādhikaraṇavacanicchā dissatīti vuttaṃ, avadhivacanicchāpi pana dissakai ‘jigucchati nāhirikau pāpā’ti, dhammā pamaj‍jatītipi hautaiva paṭisaidhābhāvā| ‘‘añ‍ñārāditararitai disāsaddāñ‍cuttarapadācāhi yuttai’’ti (2-3-29) suttitaṃ, tadāha-‘añ‍ñasaddayaugai’c‍cādi, yatauti daivadattāditau, ‘‘añ‍ñaityatthaggahaṇaṃ, taina pariyāyappayaugaipi bhavatī’’ti añ‍ñaihi byākhyātattā āha-‘bhin‍nau daivadattā’ti, ārāsaddassa dūratthattā āsavakkhayatau ārā dūrau bhavatī(tyatthau u) pādīyataityāha‘ārāsaddau dūratthau’ti, niddissamānapaṭiyaugīti ‘daivadattau sūrau’ti niddissamānassa kassaci yau sau dutiyau, sau paṭiyaugī| disādaisakālavuttīnaṃ disāsaddattaina pariggahatau āha‘disā daisakālavācīnaṃ yaugai’ti, pubbaiva sambaudhātiādīni kālavācīyaugai udāharaṇāni, añ‍cuttarapadaācaāhipac‍cayayau gaitviha naudāhaṭaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍camīvidhānai lyap‍laupai kammanyupasaṅkhyānaṃ ‘‘adhikaraṇai caupasaṅkhyāna’’miti (2-3-28-vā) vāttikaṃ, tadāha‘yattha’c‍cādi, lyappac‍cayassa yatrāppayaugau, tassatthau cai gamyatai, sau lyap‍laupau, tvālaupau lyapalaupautyavisaisau, pāsāda māruyha paikkhati āsanai upavisitvā paikkhatīti idaṃ visayau padassanaṃ pubbaṃ yassāti kiriyāvisaisanai samāsau, vakkhamānasabbadassanaisupi pāsādatau-pak‍kamanalaisasabbhāvā pāsādau (yaivā) vadhirūpaina vattumiṭṭhauti dassaitumāha-‘tathāhi’c‍cādi, apak‍kamati tatau taṃ dassananti tatau pāsādatau tassa daṭṭhunau dassanaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ apak‍kamatyapayātītyatthau, avadhibhāvau pāsādassāti viñ‍ñāyati, yaisanti vaisaisikānaṃ, tai hyaivamācikkhanti ‘‘indriyaisu cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhugauḷakatau-c‍cantasukhumabhāvaina nikkhamma visālībhavantaṃ visayadaisamupagamma sakalamattanā diṭṭhaṃ passati raṃsirūpaina, yathā kāhalā mūlai kisā aggaimahatī, tathā ñāṇindriyānī’’ti, adhiṭṭhānadaisanti indriyādhiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ cakkhugauḷakadaisaṃ, visayappavattīti gaucarai  pavatti, santatiyāti indriyapabandhassa, adhiṭṭhāna manindriyaṃ nāpaj‍jataiti sambandhau, tatauti pāsādatau, apak‍kantyāti apagamanaina, yaj‍japi cakkhundriyaṃ cakkhugauḷakatau bahi niggacchati, tathāpipāsādaṭṭhassacakkhugauḷakā niggacchatīti pāsādassāpyavadhibhāvau, yaisantūti khaṇikavādīnantu, indriyādhiṭṭhānampattai visayai viñ‍ñāṇalakkhaṇaṃ kāriyaṃ karaunti sīlaināti pattakārīni, anindriyādhiṭṭhānadausau naiti sambandhau, tatauti pāsādatau, yaisampanāti khaṇikavādinauva dassaiti, atra hi cattārau pakkhā pattakārīnindriyāni, appattakārīni, cakkhusautāni appattakārīni saisāni vuttaviparitāni, cakkhuyaiva pattakārī saisamañ‍ñathaiti, catusvaiva taisu paṭhamapakkhadvayamupanyasya paṭipāditaṃ… tappaṭipādanainaitaraisampi paṭipāditattā, sabbasāmayikasādhāraṇattāsaddasatthassāti idaṃ ayuttamiva dissati… saddasatthassimassa buddhavacanaupayaugittaina saugatasamayānuguṇappavattitau, khaṇikavādinau pana nissāyaidaṃ sundaramaiva, manasikārādītiādisaddaina cakkhutau cakkhunā (pautthakai) abhaidau rūpāpāthagamau ca dassitau, yauggadaisaiti dassanayauggai padaisai| ‘‘pucchanākhyānaisu ca pañ‍camī vattabbā’’ti (2-3-28vā) vuttaṃ, tadāha-‘pucchanasaddatau’c‍cādi, pucchanākhyānaisu sāmañ‍ñaina pañ‍camīvidhānai-tippasaṅgaṃ dassaitumāha-‘avassa’mic‍cādi, kassāti pañhau, daivadattassaityākhyānaṃ, kausau yañ‍ña dattauti pañhau vidhirūpauvāyaṃ, yau-ṅgadītyādyanuvādarūpamākhyānaṃ, aṅgadīkaiyūrī| ‘‘yataucāddhakālanimmānaṃ tatau pañ‍camī vattabbā’’ti (2-3-28vā) vuttaṃ, tadāha-‘addha’c‍cādi, nimīyatai paricchij‍jatai yaina taṃ nimānaṃ, tadaiva nimmānaṃ| ‘‘addhassa paṭhamā sattamī ca vattabbā’’ti (2-3-28) vuttaṃ, tatthāyamatthau ‘‘taṃ yuttāti vattatai, taṃ yuttā pañ‍camiyutthā parassāddhassa paṭhamā ca sattamī ca vattabbā’’ti, pāṭaliputtasmāti pañ‍cami yuttaṃ, aitāpi na vattabbāti dassaitumāha-‘sattayaujanānī’tiādi, atra hi yadā rājagahassa yaujanañ‍cābhaidau vattumicchīyatai, tadā pāṭaliputtasmā rājagahaṃ sattayaujanānīti, bhaidavacanicchāyantu ‘‘sabbhāvau’’tyādinā (2-34) sattamī siddhā‘sattasu yaujanaisu’ti| tathāhi  pāṭaliputtasmā sattasu yaujanaisu sati rājagahaṃbhavatītyayamatthau patīyatai, sattayaujanasambandhibhavanaṃ rājagaha (bhavana)ssa lakkhaṇambhavati| sambandhai chaṭṭhīti iminā ‘‘taṃ yuttā kālai sattamī vattabbā’’ti (2-3-28vā) vacanampaṭikkhipati| ‘‘karaṇaica thaukappakicchakatipayassā sattavacanassai’’ti (2-3-33) suttitaṃ, tadāha-‘yadā ic‍cādi, kicchādayautiādisaddaina thaukaappakatipayai saṅgaṇhāti, asattarūpanti addabbarūpaṃ, guṇai haitumhīti guṇapadatthai haitubhūtai ‘‘guṇai’’ti (2-21) pañ‍camīti atthau, yathāvuttavidhānainaiti ‘‘guṇai’’ti suttai vuttavidhānaina, ‘‘guṇai’’ti suttai vuttavidhānaina, yadātvic‍cādinā paraisaṃ viya karaṇai visuṃ vidhānai payaujanaṃ natthīti dassaiti| kattabbarūpassa calanassāti sambandhau, samānādhikaraṇattaina visaisananti yaujanā, samānādhikaraṇattaināti ca ‘rañ‍ñau purisau’tyādau asamānādhikaraṇassāpi visaisanattadassanatau vuttaṃ, samānādhikaraṇattaina visaisanabhāvau cāssa thaukaguṇānvitai calanai vattamānau thaukasaddau asattavacanauva visaisananti, tainaivākhyāyatai ‘thaukassa kammattā kammai dutiyā’ti| pañ‍camītyanuvattamānai ‘‘dūrantikatthaihi chaṭṭhī vā’’ti (2-3-34) vuttaṃ, tadāha‘dūranti katthaihi’c‍cādi, avadhisambandhalakkhaṇāti avadhisambandhāva lakkhaṇaṃ nimittamāsaṃ pañ‍camīchaṭṭhīnaṃ tā tathā vuttā, vattumiṭṭhauti iminā vacanicchāyaiva padhānattamāha, aivaṃ carahi taṃtaṃ kārakavacanicchāyamañ‍ñāpi vibhattiyau kasmā nappayuj‍jaiyyunti āha-‘laukiyā caitthā’tiādi, aittha vibhattīnaṃ niyamai laukiyā aiva vacanicchā nibandhanaṃ kāraṇanti atthau, pañ‍camīcātyanuvattamānai ‘‘dūranti katthaihi dutiyā cai’’ti (2-3-35) vuttaṃ, ‘‘pañ‍camyadhikaraṇaica’’ (2-3-36) ic‍catra ca kāraina dūranti katthaihi sattamī ca vuttā pāṭi padikatthai sārasaṅgahakārādīhi, tadakkhippa añ‍ñāpadaisaina paṭikkhipituṃ kaicityādi yaṃ vuttaṃ vuttiyaṃ, taṃ dassaitumāha’kaicīti sārasaṅgahādayau’tiādi| dūrāpādāvasaicananti ayaṃ taisampaṭikkhaipana yauti sambandhau, itisaddau panaittha parihīnau, nahitantiādīsu tasaddaināsattarūpaṃ dūrādi vattumicchitantyavasaiyaṃ, na hi taṃ gamyatai, na hi tatau gamyatai, na hi taina gamyatai, na hi tasmiṃ ṭhīyatai’ti visuṃ sambandhau  vasaiyau| patīyantaicāti cau vattabbantarasamuc‍cayai, ataucaiti vakkhamānahaituparāmāsau, yanti yasmā atthai nipātau, vākyakārau vararuci, taṃsāmānādhikaraṇyatauti dūrādisā mānādhikaraṇyatau, taṃyuttataupīti dūrādiyuttā gāmādisaddataupi, pac‍cakkhantauti vākyakāravacanaṃ pac‍cācikkhantau, tathāhyatrāyaṃ mahābhāssa pāṭhau ‘‘dūrantikatthaihi pañ‍camīvidhānai taṃyuttā pañ‍camīpaṭisaidhau vattabbau dūrā gāmassa, tatrāpi dassanā anatthakau-yampaṭisaidhau, tatrāpi hi pañ‍camī dissati–

dūrasmāvasathā muttaṃ, dūrāpādāvasaicanaṃ|

gurutau kupitā dūrā,bhābyaṃ dūrā ca dassuhiti||

atra dūrantikaityādi vattabbaṃ vākyakāravacanaṃ, dūrā gāmassāti tassaudāharaṇaṃ, tatrāpic‍cādi pac‍cakkhānabhāssaṃ, dūrasmāvasathāti āvasathassa dūraiti atthauhi bhāssapadīpai kaiyyaṭau, paṭhamau purisau yassa sau paṭhamapurisau bhavati ‘‘dūrantikā’’di (2-6-5) kac‍cāyanasuttai suddhādiyaugaupi saṅgahitau, taināha–‘suddhappa maucanavivittappamāṇayaugai’ti, ‘‘dhātunāmānamupasaggayaugādīsvapica’’ iti (2-6-2) suttai caggahaṇatau pabhutiyaugau ca gahitau, taināha–‘pabhuti yaugaicā’ti, pabhutiyaugaicāti iminā pabhutyatthayaugaupi saṅgahitau, tainaiva ‘yatausarāmi attāna’nti udāhaṭaṃ|

27. apa

nanu phuṭauva taisaṃ sālādīnaṃ yaugauti kimaittha pañhaina kassa panaitaihi yaugau’tyāsaṅkiyāha-‘vaj‍janaṃ hic‍cādi, vaj‍janānanti kattari anau, vaj‍janai sambandhai vattamānaihīti vaj‍janasaṅkhātai sambandhai vatta mānaihi apaparīhi, yuttasmāti yuttatauva aikasmā, nāñ‍ñatau, kimaittha vattabbanti iminā apaparīhi yuttasmāva pañ‍camībhavanai pasiddhattamāha, pasiddhampaitamāharitvā pakāsaitumāha-‘kattunau’c‍cādi, yadatthauti yausālādi atthau payaujanaṃ yassa vaj‍janakiriyā rambhassa  sau yadatthau| ‘‘pañ‍camyapāparīhī’’ti (2-3-10) āyuttaipi pañ‍camī vidhīyatai paraihi, tadāha-‘mariyādāya’mic‍cādi, avadhimā aittha vassanaṃ|

28. paṭi

diṭṭhasambandhassāti pasiddhavasaina diṭṭhakāriyakāraṇassa mukhyassa, tassa kāriyatthanti tassa mukhyassa yaṃ kāriyaṃ, tadatthaṃ mukhyakāriya karaṇatthanti vuttaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ paṭinidhimhi vattamānaina pabhinaiti sambandhau, vinimayaiti parivattana nimittaṃ paṭidānaicāti ca saddassāṭṭhānappayutti daṭṭhabbā, patinā tassa yaugauti sambandhau|

29. ritai

yadi vaj‍janakiriyāya sambajjhamānassa kammattā kammai dutiyā, kimatthaṃ carahi puna dutiyāvacananti āha-‘paṭipadavidhānai’c‍cādi, paṭipadaṃ ujukamaivauc‍cāritaṃ vidhānaṃ yassā sā paṭipadavidhānā, visaisavidhāna vatītyatthau, yaj‍jatra dutiyāvacanaṃ na siyā tadā ritaisaddaina yaugai ujukaṃ vidhīyamānāya pañ‍camiyā dutiyā bādhīyaithāti mañ‍ñatai| yadyatra pañ‍camiyā dutiyāya bādhābhāvau aiva payaujanaṃ, aivañ‍carahi ‘ritai vā’ti suttayitabbaṃ aivaṃ sati pakkhai dutiyā ca bhavissati, lahu ca suttampaṇītambhavatī tyāsaṅkiya payaujanantaramāha-‘uttaratthañ‍cai’ti, atauyaivāti uttaratthattāyaiva, puna payaujanantaraṃdutiyā gahaṇassa dassayamāha-‘vibhatyantarai’c‍cādi, āhac‍ca kaṇṭhādiṭṭhānai vāyunāāhanitvā āharitvā vā vacanaṃ āhac‍cavacanaṃ, ujukavacananti vuttaṃ hauti, vibhatyantaraṃ chaṭṭhīvibhatti, tassa byudāsatthaṃ, nirāsatthantutthau, añ‍ñāpīti dutiyāya añ‍ñā chaṭṭhīpi, pakatāya pañ‍camiyā sambandhanatthau pakatasambandhanatthau|

31. putha

na dutiyāpi yaugavibhāgatauti aivaṃ mañ‍ñatai ‘‘tatiyā caiti vattamānai tattha yadi cakāraina ‘dutiyā cai’ti sambandhīyaiyya, tatrāpi ca kāraina pañ‍camītyaivaṃ na kassāpyapakaṃsauti‘vināñ‍ñatraputhanānāhi  tatiyā cai’tyaikayaugamakatvā yaugavibhāgasāmatthiyā ‘tatiyā cai’ti ca kāraina jātu pañ‍camī’’ti, asahāyatthattai sati ‘vinātthai hī’tyaivamavatvā kiṃ visuṃ suttaracanāyaityāha-‘bhaidau pādānantvi’c‍cādi|

32. satta

yantaṃ saddānaṃ nic‍cattā sambandhassa yatthāti parāmaṭṭhaṃ sauti, yauti parāmaṭṭhaṃ tassāti ca niddisati, yau yatthaityādikañ‍ca ‘kiriyādhārabhūtai’c‍cādivuttigantha vivaraṇanti yauti kiriyādhaiyyabhūtādhippaitā, aitanaivāha‘kiriyā cā’tiādi, taināyamādhārīyati kattukamma samavaitā kiriyāsmintyadhikaraṇai vā taṃ ādhārayatīti kattari vā ghaṇantaina daṭṭhabbau, kattāraṃ kammañ‍ca kiriyānissayabhūtamādhāra yatītyādhārauti vā kattari ghaṇa, vuttaṃ hi–

ādhārayati yau kattu, kammaṃ kiriyanissayaṃ|

ādhāraṃ kārakañ‍cāhu, taṃ catubbidhamaiva caiti||

nanu ca kiriyādhāraṇainādhārautyuc‍catai, taṃ kimaivamuc‍cataiti cai, naisadausau yadaiva hi kiriyādhāraṇamassa, tantan‍nissayabhūta kattukammadhāraṇaṃ, nahyañ‍ñatā taddhāraṇaṃ sambhavati tatau kiriyā kāraṇainādhāraṇainādhārautya yamaivatthau, na kattukammaparic‍cāgainādhāraina ujukaṃ kiriyā gayhatai, tainaiva vakkhati-‘kiriyāyayau ādhārau’c‍cādi, nanu yadyapyujukamaiva kiriyaṃ dhārayati kattā kattusamavāyiniṃ, kammasamavaitañ‍ca viktaidanādiṃ kiriyaṃ kammaṃ… tathā sati tānaivādhārauti, na… laukai satthai ca taisamādhāratthai nāppatītitau, kattukammatāyaiva hi taisampatītīti dassaitumāha-‘kiriyā cai’tyādi, tathā ca vuttaṃ–

kattukamma byavahitaṃ, dhāraintamanuju kriyaṃ|

kriyāsiddhupakāri ca, satthai-dhikaraṇaṃ matanti||

kattukammabyavahitanti kattukammaihi taṃsamavaitabhāvaina byavahitaṃ, anuju pārampariyaina, kriyāsiddhupakāriti kattukammasamavaitāya kiriyāya pasiddhiyaṃ upakārī upakubbantaṃ, nanu ca na kiriyā dhāraṇainaivāyamādhārau, kattukammānaṃ dhāraṇaināpyādhārauti sak‍kā  vavatthapaitunti caudanammanasi nidhāyāha-‘na cai’tyādi, kiriyā nimittassaivāti kattukammasamavāyiniyā kiriyāya nimittassaiva, tadaivaṃvidhamādhāraṃ catubbidhammatanti dassaitumāha-‘ādhārau cāya’mic‍cādi, cattārau vidhāpakārā assāti catubbidhau, ādhaiyyaina sahaupasilaisau saṃyaugalakkhaṇau-ssa atthīti aupasilaisikau ādhārau-‘kaṭainisīdati thāliyaṃ pacati’tyādau kaṭādi, tathāhi daivadattasamavaitaṃ nisīdanakiriyaṃ kaṭau daivadattaṃ dhārayaṃ dhārayati daivadattabyavahitaṃ, aivaṃ thālīpi taṇḍulasamavaitaṃ vik‍laidanādikiriyaṃ taṇḍulai dhārayaṃ dhārayatītyaupasilaisikādhārattaṃ kaṭādinau| yatau-ñ‍ñatrādhaiyyaṃ na vattatai sabbathā bhiyyau vā, sau vaisayikau, visayauhyādhaiyyassānañ‍ñatrabhāvau, yathā cakkhādippavattīnamañ‍ñatrābhāvā cakkhādīnaṃ rūpādayau visayāti vuc‍cantai, aivamākāsai sakunayautyādau sakunyādīnamākāsāditau-ñ‍ñatrābhāvātai taisaṃ visayātyuc‍cantai, visayau aiva vaisayikau| yau ādhārādhaiyyānamabhibyāpaityādhaiyyamityabhibyāpakau, tathā hi‘tilaisu tailaṃ, dadhimhi sappi,tyatra tilādikaṃ tailādikamādhaiyyaṃ byāpaitvā tiṭṭhatīti tilādi byāpakādhārau| samīpai bhavau, samīpau aiva vā sāmīpikau, yassa samīpadaisai ādhaiyyaṃ vattatai sau aivamuc‍catai, atraudāharaṇaṃ ‘gurūsu vasati gaṅgāyaṃ ghausau’ti| nanu cātrāpyādhārai sattamī vidhīyatai ādhārau ca nissayau vuc‍catai, nissayau ca saṃyaugasamavāyaihi hauti, na ca sissādīnaṃ guruppabhutīti daivadattasakunyādīnamiva kaṭākāsādīhi saṃyaugau, tailādīnamiva ca tilādīhi samavāyau vā atthi, taināyuttaṃ tai samādhārattanti, naitadatthi, yadāyattā hi yassa ṭhiti sau vināpi saṃyaugasamavāyaihi tassa nissayau bhavati, tathāhi purisassa na rañ‍ñā saha saṃyaugausamavāyau vātthi, atha pana tadadhīnaṭṭhitittā rājanissayau purisauti byapadissīyatai, sissādīnañ‍ca guruppabhutina māyattā ṭhitīti taisamayaṃ tainassāyaṃ (pautthakai) yutthau tai pati gurupabhutīnaṃ nissayabhāvau (jinindabuddhi) yuttau guruppabhutinaṃ nissayabhāvauti, aittha  pana yaj‍japi vaisayikābhibyapakānamādhārādhaiyyaupasilaisau atthi, tathāpi pakārantarassaiva paripphuṭattā tathaiva vacanicchā, nāñ‍ñathā aupasilaisikaina vā aupasilaisikaiva visayābhibyāpakānamaggahaṇatau upasilaisassai vā upasilaisassaiva paribyattattā tathaiva vauhārauti na saṅkarau saṅkanīyauti|

33. nimitta

nimittabhāvamattaitīminā kammasaṃyaugābhāvaipi sattamiyā pavatti māha, na paraisaṃ viya kammasaṃyaugai aiva, tainaiva vakkhati-‘aivamañ‍ñatai’c‍cādi, bāhirānaṃ hi-‘nimittā kammaina’’ (caṃ, 2-1-89) iti suttaṃ, tattha kammaina yaugai payaujanā sattamībhavatītyatthau, ajinamhi hañ‍ñatai dīpītidīpinauhananassa payaujanaṃ cammaṃ, tassa dīpinaṃhantīti kammaina yau gau, suttai kammainātyavacanai payaujanaṃ vattumārabhatai ‘na panai’c‍cādi|

34. yabbhā

yassa kiriyāti yassa gavādyatthassa sambandhinī dauhanādikiriyā, kiriyantarassāti gamanādikiriyantarassa, aitaināti lakkhaṇanti aitaina, tatau-c‍cassa vivaraṇaṃ kiriyāvatāti, ‘‘kālabhāvaisu cai’’ti (2-6-43) kac‍cāyanaihi suttitaṃ, tadāha-‘kālatthaihi cā’tiādi|

35. chaṭṭhī

sāmissarādiyaugai chaṭṭhīsattamiyau yathā siyunti ‘‘sāmissarādhipatidāyādasakkhipatibhūpasutaihi ca ‘‘iti (2-3-39) āraddhaṃ paraihi, tadāha-‘sāmi’c‍cādi, atra ca jinindabuddhinā ‘‘sāmyādīnaṃ bhaidainau pādānaṃ pariyāyantaranivatyatthamiha mā hautu gāmassa rājā’’ti vuttaṃ vākyamupanyasya hassāpya yuttattamupadassayamāha-‘sāmissarai’c‍cādi, kaina nivārīyatai, na kaināpītyatthau, visayavacanicchāyaṃ ‘gāmai rājā’tyapi bhavitabbamityanaināha, ‘‘āyuttakusalaihyāsaivāya’’mīti (2-3-40) āraddhaṃ, tadāha-‘āyuttai’c‍cādi, āsavā tapparatā, byāvaṭau niyuttau, tatthaidaṃ siyā ‘‘āsaivāyanti vakkhāmī tyavassaṃ  vacanamidamārabbhanīyamañ‍ñathā īsaṃyuttaipi ‘āyuttau gau sakaṭassā’ti pasaj‍jatī’’tyāsaṅkiya natthaivaitampi payaujananti dassaitumāha-‘vināpi’c‍cādi, ‘‘sāmissarā’’tyadikaṃ kac‍cāyanasuttaṃ, tainaivāti kac‍cāyanasuttai pasūtasaddānantaraṃ kusalasaddaniddaisainaiva, idhāti mauggal‍lānavuttiyaṃ| kac‍cāyanaina ‘‘kammakaraṇa nimittatthaisu sattamī’’ (2-6-40) ‘‘pañ‍camyatthai ca’’ (2-6-41) iti ca suttitaṃ, taināha-‘kammai’c‍cādi, gahaṇassa bāhā visayauti sambandhau, kac‍cāyanānaṃ‘bhikkhūsū’tiādīni kammatthai udāharaṇāni, hatthaisūtiādīni karaṇatthai, kadalīsūti pañ‍camyatthai| ‘‘maṇḍitussuk‍kaisu tatiyā cai’’ti (2-6-45) kac‍cāyanasuttaṃ, tadāha ‘pasan‍natthai’c‍cādi, pasan‍natthaiti maṇḍitatthai, ussuk‍katthaiti ussāhatthai|

36. yatau

visuṃ karaṇaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ, jinindabuddhācariyaina ‘‘kimatthampunaridaṃ vacanaṃ yāvatā niddhāriyamānau-vayavau samudāyabbhantarau, tattha yadā samudāyassā-dhikaraṇattaṃ vattumicchīyatai, tadā sattamī siddhāva, yathā rukkhai sākhāti, yadā tvavayavasambandhau tadā chaṭṭhī, yathā rukkhassa sākhāti sac‍camaitaṃ papañ‍catthantu vacana’’nti vuttaṃ, tañ‍caita mayuttantidassaintau āha-‘na sālayau’c‍cādisūkayuttānidhañ‍ñāni sūkadhañ‍ñāni, āhitā avaṭṭhitā, ādhaiyya bhūtāti vuttaṃ hauti| yatauti āhitattakāraṇā, tatauti yatau jinindabuddhivacanamaivamayuttaṃ, tasmā kāraṇā| ‘pañ‍camī vibhattai’’ti (2-3-42) pāṇinīya suttaṃ, tadāha-‘samudāyatau’c‍cādi, sutatau sakāsā sīlaṃ atisayaina saiyyauti sambandhau, ‘‘niddhāraṇai chaṭṭhī sattamīsu pattaisu tadapavādau, yaṃ yaugau’’ti paraihākhyāyatai, tattha tāva niddhāraṇamaivaittha natthi kutau chaṭṭhīsattamīnaṃ tatthappavatti, yatau tāsaṃ bādhanatthamidamārabbhataiti dassayamāha-‘nātra jātyādīhi’c‍cādi, nahic‍cādinā jātyādīhi niddhāraṇābhāvaṃ dassaiti, jac‍cādīnanti niddhāraṇai chaṭṭhī, kutau na bhavatic‍cāha-‘jac‍cādisambandhainai’c‍cādi, ataṃ byapadaisatauti sīlattādibyapadaisābhāvatau, puna niddhāraṇābhāvai haitvantaramāha-‘tulyānañ‍cai’’c‍cādi| sādhunipuṇaihi yaugai pūjāyaṃ gammamānāyaṃ sattamī, tathā asādhuyaugai, tathā pasitaussuk‍kaihi yaugai tatiyāsattamiyau, tathā laupantanakkhattasaddā ca taina taina vacanantaraina pāṇiniyaihi vihitā, taṃ sabbaṃ taṃtaṃkārakavacanicchāyamaiva sādhaituṃ ‘mātari sādhu’ ic‍cādayau udāhaṭā kaisaisu pasitau pasattau, avabaddhauti atthau, kaisaihi karaṇabhūtaihi pasitau bhavati, kattubhūtaihi vā pasitau katauti atthau, phussaininduyuttaina lakkhitau kālau sauyamityabhaidaina sambandhā phussai, taina karaṇabhūtaina|

37. paṭhamā

paṭhamātthamattaiti aittakai vuttaipi vākyatthau padatthau vā na viñ‍ñāyatai nāmaggahaṇānuvattitauti dassaitumāha-‘dvaidvaikānaikādi suttatau’tiādi, nāmainātthau paṭipādiyamānau nāmasaṅkhātai-ttani abhidhaiyyattainājjhāraupya paṭipādayatyanapaikkhita vibhattivisaisanti āha-‘abhidhīyati’c‍cādi, pakatirūpaināti nāmaina, tassa abhidhaiyyassa, mattasaddassātiādinā sāmañ‍ñantīdaṃ mattasaddassatthavacananti dassaiti, idaṃ vuttaṃ hautīti nāmassābhidhaiyyamattaiti iminā idaṃ vakkhamānaṃ vuttaṃ hauti| ‘‘pāṭipadikatthaliṅgaparimāṇavacanamattai paṭhamā’’ti (2-3-46) liṅgādīsu bhaidaina paṭhamāvidhānampaṭipan‍nā, tadāha-‘liṅgaparimāṇai’c‍cādi, liṅgaṃ itthipun‍napuṃsakāni, parimāṇaṃ paricchaidau, saṅkhyā aikattabahuttāni, tabbantaupi saddatthauaivāti liṅgādivantaupi sarūpā byatirittau saddatthau aiva, uc‍cattanīcattasāmañ‍ñampuraudhāya pavattiyā uc‍canīcasaddaihi paṭhamā hautaiva, taṃyuttassāpi saddatthabhāvatau ‘uc‍caṃ gharāni’tyādīnipi uc‍cattaṃ guṇaṃ nimittaṃ katvā sauyamityabhaidasambandha gharaisu vattantīti| palambatai ajjhāgacchatityādau ‘‘asaṅkhyaihi sabbāsaṃ’’ti (2-119) laupavidhānasāmatthiyā pādīhyanatthakaihipi paṭhamā siddhāyaiva|

38. āmanta

abhimukhaṃ katvātiādinā yaugārambhassa phalaṃ dassaiti, nāmatthaiti  saddatthai, padatauti āmantaṇādhikaatthamattappakāsakapadatau, haunti caittha…

siddhassābhimukhīkāra bhimukhībhāva (pautthakai), mattamāmantaṇaṃ siyā|

atthau katābhimukhau hi, kriyāyaṃ viniyuj‍jatai|

āmantaṇaṃ na vākyatthau, padatauva patītitau|

natthaivāmantaṇaṃ laukai, vidhātabbaina vatthunā|

taṃ yathā bhava rājaiti, nippan‍natthau bhavaiti ca||

siddhassāti daivadattattādinā siddhassa, atthauti daivadattādi, kataṃ ābhimukhyaṃ samādhānaṃ yaināti viggahau, kriyāyaṃ viniyuj‍jatai gaccha bhuñ‍jāti, āmantaṇaṃ natthi… idāni vidhīyamānattaināladdhasattikassābhimukhayitumasak‍karūpattā, vidhātabbaināti nipphādaitabbaina|

39. chaṭṭhī

kiriyākārakaic‍cādivuttiganthassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattumārabhatai ‘aikāyaṃ sambandhau nāmai’c‍cādi, kiriyāya kārakaihi ca nipphāditauti iminā sambandhassa kiriyākārakasambandha pubbakattamāha, tatauyaivāyaṃ nākasmīkauti na yaisaṃkaisañ‍ci dvin‍naṃ bhavati, visiṭṭhānaṃyaiva tu dvin‍naṃ bhavatītyatippasaṅgābhāvau, kiriyākārakasambandhapubbakau hi kārakaihi añ‍ñau sassāmibhāvādikau sambandhau, tathā ca vuttaṃ–

‘‘sambandhau kārakaihañ‍ñau, kriyākārakapubbakau|

sutāyamassutāyaṃvā, kriyāyaṃ sau-bhidhīyataiti||

tattha assutāyaṃ kiriyāyaṃ ‘rañ‍ñau purisau’c‍cādau kiriyā kārakasambandhapubbakau-ñ‍ñau aiva sassāmibhāvādikau sambandhau patīyataiti, ‘sarati raj‍jassa’tyādau tu suyyamānai kiriyāsaddai, aittha pana santamapi kammaṃ vattumanicchitaṃ, visaisanabhāvauyaiva saraṇampati raj‍jassa paṭipādīyatai’raj‍jasambandhisaraṇa’nti, kiriyākāraka sambandhau hi sabbattha vatthuṭṭhitivasainaivatthi, tan‍nimittau ca sassāmi bhāvādi, tattha sassāmibhāvavivacchāyaṃ vij‍jamānaupi kiriyākāraka sambandhau  na vattumicchitau, yathā ‘anudarā kañ‍ñā’ti, sau cāyaṃ sambandhau sassāmibhāvajañ‍ñajanakabhāvā-vayavāvayavavibhāvādilakkhaṇau bahuvidhauti vaiditabbau, tattha sassāmisambandhai rañ‍ñau purisau, jañ‍ñajanakasambandhai nigraudhassa bījaṃ, avayavāvayavisambandhai rukkhassa sākhā, idāni sambandhassaitassa kiriyākārakasambandhasañ‍jātattaṃ yathāyaugamañ‍ñatrāpyavagamayituṃ kiñ‍ci udāharaṇamāha-‘tathā hi’c‍cādi, paripālanakiriyākatau naisaṃ sambandhauti iminā paripālyaparipālanalakkhaṇau-yaṃ sambandhauti dassaiti, sā ayampari pālanalakkhaṇā kiriyā sambandhamassaidambhāvahaituṃ janayitvā nivattati, atthappakaraṇādinā tvimāya kiriyāyāyaṃ sambandhauti visaisau vasīyatai, na chaṭṭhiyā visaisāvagamanai sāmatthiyaṃ… tassā sabbatthaikarūpattā, suyyamānai aiva vā kiriyāsaddai kārakabhāvassa haitunau vacanicchāyābhāvai assaidambhābhāvasaṅkhātassaiva phalassa vattumicchitattā tathaiva sambandhau jātau chaṭṭhiyābhidhīyatai ‘raj‍jassa saratī’ti, vuttaṃ hi–

janayitvāna sambandhaṃ, kriyā kāci nivattati|

suyyamānai kriyāsaddai, sambandhau jāyatai k‍vacīti||

paripālayatīti purisamparipālayati, tatau pālanādikā upakāra sabhāvā kiriyādvin‍nampi taisaṃ sambandhinī bhavati, rañ‍ñā hi kāriyamānā taṃsambandhinīpurisavisayattāvapurisayauginītyubhin‍nampikiriyāsambhavati, tatauyaivatassā ubhayasambandhiniyā kiriyāya sambhavaiassaidanti buddhiyā haitubhūtassa añ‍ñamañ‍ñāpaikkhasabhāvassa sambandhassa bhāvatau kiriyājanitattamassa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñāpaikkhālakkhaṇau… sabbathā nirapaikkhattai sambandhābhāvā, sambandhi buddhisabbhavassāti sambandhīsu rājapurisādīsu assaidanti aivampavattāya buddhiyā sabbhavassa| paraisaṃ viya ‘‘guṇai chaṭṭhī’’ti vacanantarābhāvai ubhayatthapi (caittha) (yaiva) (pautthakai) chaṭṭhippa- saṅgauti baudhaitumāha-‘nanu cai’c‍cādi, sac‍canti yathā vuttaṃ caudanamabbhupagamma caudakampati yathāvuttaṃ sambandharūpaṃ tatau-ñ‍ñatrāpyupasaṃ haritvā  tattha chaṭṭhippasaṅgaṃ caudaitumāha-‘daivadattau’c‍cādi, athaiva mic‍cādināsatīpiañ‍ñamañ‍ñāpaikkhālakkhaṇai sambandhai vacanicchāyaivaittha kāraṇanti caudakādhippāyamatraupanaitvā tadaiva visaisatau chaṭṭhiyā abhāvai samupanaituṃ‘yaj‍jaivamihāpi’c‍cādikamāha, tattha athāti caudakādhippāyappakāsanārambhai nipātau, aivaṃ vattabbanti aivaṃ yadipic‍cādinā vakkhamānanayaina vattabbaṃ, paṭipādaitabbatāyātyādinā idandīpaiti ‘‘visaissaṃ vidhaiyatāya na parādhīnanti appadhānabhāvatau byatiric‍catai tatau-ttaniṭṭhamaiva, na rūpantarambhajati‘purisassai’ti, visaisanantu rājādikaṃ tadaṅgatāyānuvadiyamānamappadhānattā kārakarūpatau byatiric‍catai, tatau rūpantaraṃ bhajati ‘rañ‍ñau’ti, tatau byatiraika lakkhaṇau sambandhau hauti, aikassa sambandhittāsambhavā vatthutau dviṭṭhau visaisanagatattaina patīyatai tatau chaṭṭhī visaisanamaivānudhāvati patītivisayai saddappavattī’’ti, yau hi vikappanaṃ visayau sau saddānanti, atauyaiva sambandhau niyamaina visaisanabhaidamanuvidadhāti… vatthutau dviṭṭhassāpi taggatattaina patītiyā yathā bhātūnaṃ dhananti, nāvassambhātūnaṃ dhanantic‍caiva bhavati, tathā satyapi visaissa bhaidai visaisanagatamaiva bhaidamanu(vidadhā)ti ‘daivadattassa assā’ti, tadaitaṃ sabbaṃ visaisanagatattaina sambandhapatītitau-vakappīyatai, visaissagatattaināpi tappatītiyaṃ niyamaina taggatabhaidānuvidhānaṃ siyā visaisanagatabhaidānuvidhānamiva, dviṭṭhattantu vatthutau cattunau na virujjhatai, vattupaṭibaddhamaiva ca dviṭṭhattaṃ manasikatvā taṃvādīhi dviṭṭhau sambandhaubhidhīyatai, atauyaivāyaṃ byatiraikavibhatti (yāgamyatai)… byatiric‍camānavisaisanabhūtasambandhigatabyatiraikalakkhaṇasambandhābhi- dhānappavattitauti, nīlādivisaisanantu paratthattaināppadhānampi na kārakabhāvatau byatiric‍cati… nīlādisaddainuppalādidabbābhidhānatau, yathā ‘nīlamuppalampassa, nīlainuppalaina ṭhitaṃ, nīlassuppalassa gandhau’ti, yadā tu na visaissapavatti tadā byatiric‍cataiva ‘nīlassa guṇassa uppalan‍nissayau’ti kārakabhāvalakkhaṇā padhānabhāvā byatiric‍catai aiva nīlādi, tatau visaisanamaivānaikappakāratāyāva tiṭṭhatai, tatau visai- sanaṃ  kiñ‍cidaiva kārakarūpatau padhānabhāvā byatiric‍cataiti visaisana mattaināpi saisaṃ sabbaṃ tathā siyāti nāsaṅkanīyanti|

dvaipīti rājapurisā dvaipi, tatiyai gaihasaṅkhātai sambandhinī| sahayuttampaṭic‍ca aivanti iminā purisassa appadhānattamaiva dīpaiti, abhimatāti pāṇiniyānaṃ suttantaraina abhimatā, yatau chaṭṭhīya-mattanau matai sambandhanissayā, taināha- ‘sambandhamaivi’c‍cādi, sambandhau caittha mātu saraṇānamavaṭṭhānādikiriyānimittauti kaici, añ‍ñai tu saraṇassa kiriyārūpattā kiriyantaramantarainaiva dabbaina sambandhaupapattimāhuyathā dvin‍naṃ jatukatau saṃsilaisau, jatunau tu kaṭṭhaināttanāyaiva, na jatvantarakatau saṃsilaisauti| paraisaṃ tatiyatthai dutiyatthai ca kitakappayaugai ñ‍ñatrāpi (pañ‍camyatthai) sattamyatthai ca (‘‘chaṭṭhī cā’’) ti (2-6-20) dutiyā pañ‍camīnañ‍cā’’ti (2-6-29) ca suttantaraihi chaṭṭhītimatā, tassāpi vatticchātauva sādhitabhāvaṃ dassaitumāha-yathācaitthātiādi, aitthāpyādau udāharaṇadvayai suttantaramantaraina sampadānattābhāvā sambandhavacanicchāyamaiva paraisampi chaṭṭhī, taināha-‘aitthā’tiādi, bahulantīmassa atthaṃ dassaiti ‘yathā dassana’nti, avisaṃvādakālaukassatyādikaṃ kammai udāharaṇaṃ, pañ‍camyatthai pana vatticchātauva sabbai tasanti daṇḍassātyādīni udāharaṇāni vaiditabbāni|

40. tulya

anabhimatāti ‘tulyatthaihyatulaupamāhi tatiyāñ‍ñatarissa’nti (2-3-72) suttaina tulaupamāhi añ‍ñaihi tulyatthaihi yaugai tatiyāya vidhīyamānattā pāṇiniyānaṃ anabhimatā, yasmā sadisā bhāvā taisaṃ naupamāti ‘aj‍junassa tulā natthī’tyādikamuc‍catai, tasmā aivaṃ yathāvuttanayaināti tadaivantīmassa atthau vaiditabbau, tadaivanti vā nipātasamudāyau yathāvuttanidassanatthau, tulyaisūti sadisaisu, tathāvuttāti aj‍junassatulānatthītyādinā taina pakāraina vuttā|

41. atau

suttai  ‘atau’ti vuttaṃ ‘akārantatau’ti kathaṃ vuttīti āha ‘atau’tiādi, ādaisā hauntīti saisau, yakārānaṃyaiva siyuṃ… āaiādaisānamaikavaṇṇattā, laupai kataiti aukārassa laupai katai, tan‍naiti yathāvuttaṃ dhacādyappaṭikkhipati, paṭikkhaipai kāraṇamāha-‘paralaupassā’tiādi, kāraṇantaraṃ vattumārabhatai ‘kiñ‍cā’tiādi, nic‍camparalaupauva siyā, na pubbalaupaupi āgamappayaugānukūlyainātipi sak‍kā vattunti āha-‘nacā’tiādi, nic‍camparalaupauyaivāti na ca sak‍kā mantunti sambandhau, aivaṃ santaityādinā tattha viraudhamāha, atausambhavāti ādaisākāratau sambhavā, jhissākāravidhānaṃ ‘‘yausu jhissa pumai’’ti (2-93) aitadaivapayaujanantiādinā vuttappayaujanābhāvai kāraṇamāha-‘aniṭṭhattā’ti, anabhimatattāti atthau, payaujanābhāvānabhimatattamaiva dassaisumāha-‘aitadatthamaivā’tiādi, imassāti ‘‘atau yaunaṃ ṭāṭai’’tī massa suttassa, jhismātvaivāti ‘jhismā’ iti aitādisaṃ suttamaiva, jhismā yaunaṃ ṭāṭaitivāti ‘‘jhismā yaunaṃ ṭāṭai’’ti suttaṃ vā, atauti ‘atau’ iti suttaṃ, tādisassapayaugassāti aggā, aggai iti payaugassa|

42. nīnaṃ

yasmā aikamaiva rūpamudāhaṭaṃ, tasmā rūpārūpāni rūpairūpānīti bhaidau vaiditabbau, taina hi bahuvacanai bahuvacanaina (pautthakai) paṭhamādutiyāsu ‘aṭṭhīnī’ti aikamaiva rūpamudāhaṭanti dassaiti|

44. sassāya

suñiti ñakārau kimatthauti āha-‘ñakārau’tiādi, asati hi ñakārai‘ñānubandhau’ti ñakāraina visaisanaina sukārau na visaisī yaiyya satiyaiva tasmiṃ taina sau visaisīyaiyya, tasmā ‘‘ñakānubandhādyantā’’ti suttai ‘ñakānubandhā’ti visaisanaṃ  visaisanabhāvau atthau payaujanaṃ yassa sau visaisanatthau, bahulaṃ vacanainānabhimatamanavasaisaṃ vavatthāpīyatīti vuttiyaṃ ‘‘bhiyyau’’tyādikamupadiṭṭhanti dassaitumupak‍kamatai ‘sāmañ‍ñainā’tiādinā, chaṭṭhī bhavatīti (sai)sau, nanuca atthasaddaināñ‍ñapadatthasamāsai kathaṃ catutthyatthau-vasīyatīti vuc‍catai, catutthīaikavacanai attattha’nti aittha saddhammasavanādikamattanimittanti yau-tthau, sau catthau-ñ‍ña padatthaipi, tassā-ñ‍ñapadatthabhūtassāpi attā atthau payaujanambhavati nimittabhāvaināti nātthabhaidauti ta aita imavaj‍jitānaṃ sabbādīnanti ta aitaimasaddaihi aiva vaj‍jitānamañ‍ñaisaṃ sabbādīnaṃ, paṭisiddhaisupi kassaci ādaisau dissatīti dassaitumāha‘tasaddassa cā’ti|

45. ghapa

itthiyā vadhuyāti pasañ‍ñānamī ūnamudāharaṇāni|

46. ssāvā

ghapasañ‍ñaihīti idaṃ taitimāmūhi tīmassa añ‍ñapadatthasamāsaina vā visaisanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, vuttiyaṃ katanti ādikaṃ kattādippakāsanatthaṃ kataṃ, tāyāti sabbattha vikappaudāharaṇaṃ|

47. namhi

‘‘āgamā tagguṇībhūtā taggahaṇaina gayhantī’’ti paribhāsā vacanamaitaṃ tattha tagguṇībhūtāti tasmiṃ āgamimhi guṇībhūtā, avayavavasaina pacchā bhavantau amukhyabhūtā, taggahaṇaināti āgamiggahaṇaina taggahaṇaina gahaṇatauti āgamibhūtassa naṃ ic‍cassa gahaṇaina gahaṇatau, ‘namhi vibhattimhi’ti tu vattabbaṃ, vipal‍lāsau vā aittha daṭṭhabbau|

(49) ikārassaivavātiādinā vuttamaiva vivarati tissa ic‍cādinā, tassa tissa, niddiṭṭhattāti tissa niddiṭṭhattā, ‘‘namhi ticatun‍namitthiyaṃ tissa catassā’’ti (2-20) tissādaisau ‘‘namhi nuka dvādīnaṃ sattarasan‍na’’nti (2-47) nuka, tissan‍naṃ|

51. suña

vibhattisuttaiti  ‘‘dvaidvaikānaikaisvi’’(2-1) c‍cādisuttai, ssananti bahuvacanaina sahitamaikavacanamāha, suttamidanti ‘‘suñsassā’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ, suttai pana satīti ‘‘suña sassā’’ti suttai sati|

55. ratyā

ratti ca ādi ca ratyādayau, taihi|

56. suhi

anukaraṇavasaināti anusadisaṃ karaṇaṃ vacanamanukaraṇaṃ|

57. ltu

taināti yatau ltuiti visaisanattaina vattumicchitassa pac‍cayassa gahaṇaṃ, taināti atthau, pac‍cayassa gahaṇā tadādigahaṇaṃ labbhatai… ‘‘pac‍cayaggahaṇai yatau sau vihitau, tadādinau gahaṇa’’nti ñāyā, visaisanattaina vattumicchitattā tadantaggahaṇaṃ… ‘‘vidhibbisaisanantassā’’ti (1-13) vacanatauti manasi nidhāyāha- ‘yatau’ic‍cādi, sau kriyatthau ādi yassa sau tadādi, sau pac‍cayau-ntau yassa samudāyassa sau tadantau, tādisassa tadā ditadantasamudāyavisaisa(ssa) gahaṇaṃ, na tu tadantamattassaityatthau, nanu ca ‘ltvī’ti visaisanattaina vattumicchitai bhavatu ‘‘vidhibbisaisanantassā’’ti tadantassa gahaṇaṃ, pac‍cayaggahaṇaina pana tadādinau gahaṇaṃ kathaṃ siyā vacanābhāvātyāha-‘tadavinābhāvittā’ti, yatau vihitau, taina vinā na bhavati sīlaināti tadavinābhāvī, tassa bhāvau tasmā| ‘‘pac‍cayaggahaṇai yatau sau vihitau tadādinau gahaṇa’’nti ñāyassa yattha pac‍cayaggahaṇaṃ tattha sādhāraṇattā ‘tadādinau’ti vuttaṃ, tadādisamudāyassa gahaṇai panaittha visaisau na dissati… adhikattāti adhigatantāvāti (pautthakai) ādīsu visaisadassanāsambhavā, ‘tuṃsmā laupau cicchāyaṃ tai’’ tyatra (5-4) tu dissati visaisau, vakkhatihi tattha ‘‘tuṃtāyaitavai bhāvai bhavissati kriyāyaṃ tadatthāya’’nti (5-61) tuṃpac‍cayaṃ vakkhati’’c‍cādi|

58. gai

aityaivavattabbanti  ‘gaia’ityaiva vattabbanti vuttaṃ hauti, pakkhai dīghavidhānainaivāti iminā akārādaisai katai pakkhai dīghavidhānainaiva, añ‍ñatthacaritatthatāyāti ltvantāditau añ‍ñattha bhaupurisā bhaupurisaic‍cādau jātipadatthanissayanaina katatthatāya, parisa mattatthatāyāti vuttaṃ hauti, avassaṃ dīghabhāvai kāraṇamāha ‘yadicākārau’ic‍cādi, yadica ākārauti padacchaidau|

60. ghabrahmā

ghaitisañ‍ñā ghasañ‍ñā tāsaṃ, kañ‍ñāsañ‍ñātiādīsu ghasañ‍ñānaṃ bahuttā bahuvacanaina niddaisau, gharūpassāti ghassāti ghasaddarūpassa, tathā bhau brahmāti aittha tathāti iminā brahmātirūpassa niruttiyaṃ niddiṭṭhattāva aittha saṅgahitabhāvaṃ dassaiti| aivaṃ vivarantīti tasmā sa sakhatau gassa akāraākārā’’tiādinā aivaṃ vivaranti, tai hi aca āca ica īca aicāti dvandai pubbasarānaṃ laupaṃ katvā aittupasilaisaniddaisaṃ vaṇṇainti, sāmañ‍ñavidhānanti ‘brahmakattuisi sakhatau’c‍caivamavisaisaitvā sāmañ‍ñaina kathanaṃ, kimatthanti kiṃ atthau payaujanaṃ yassa taṃ kimatthaṃ| sāmañ‍ñavidhānaṃ kimatthampanaivaṃ payaujanāsaṅkāyaṃ payaujanāsaṅkāvicāraṇaṃ manasi nidhāya ‘āgati gaṇauya’nti vuttattā āha– ‘atthatau’c‍cādi, atthatauti sā matthiyatau, anaināti‘āgatigaṇauya’nti iminā vacanaina, idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti ‘āgatigaṇauyanti iminā tadākārasaṅkhātajātippadhānagaṇattappakāsanaina yadi sambaudhanai aikārantākārantalābhīnaṃ kattu isi sakha muni bhadantādīnaṃ gahaṇaṃ payaujanaṃ na siyā, añ‍ñathā anupapaj‍janaṃ siyāti aivamañ‍ñathānupapattilakkhaṇasāmatthiyatau sāmañ‍ñavidhānai payaujanamāhai’’ti, sauyanti iminā vuttiyaṃ ‘āgati gaṇauya’nti vuttaṃ vacanaṃ nidassaiti|

63. ghaussaṃ

ssañ‍ca ssāca ssāyauca añ‍ca tiñ‍cāti itarītarayaugai cattha samāsau,ssaṃ ssā ssāyaṃ tiṃsu|

64. aika

ghauca  auca ghau ghāntaaukārantā, natthi ghau yaisantai aghau, taisaṃ, sāmañ‍ñavidhānatauti kassaci liṅgassa aparāmāsā sāmañ‍ñaina vidhānatau, nādaikavacanaisūti nādīsu aikavacanaisūti atthau, hi haitumhi, yasmā kassaci aivaṃ duṭṭhādhippāyau siyā, tasmā dutiyamudāharaṇanti atthau, dutiyaupi haitumhi, yasmā sabbaisaṃ saṅgahaṇatthaṃ, tasmā na ca tathādhippaitanti atthau, sabbaisamaikavacanānaṃ saṅgahaṇābhāvai suttānaṃ viracanappakāramāha-‘añ‍ñathā’tiādi, uparicātyādīsvayamadhippāyau ‘‘suttassa ‘aṃyausvaghauna’nti viracitattā sāmañ‍ñainai-kavacanaisvaghassa narassattappatīti tappaṭisaidhābhāvā’daṇḍi kula’nti rassattaṃ ‘sismiṃ napuṃsakai’ti vidhisuttaṃ vadaiyyā’’ti, aukārantappaṭisaidhanti ‘aghauna’nti aukārantappaṭisaidhaṃ, ādaisantaraṃ ‘‘gaussā gasihinaṃ sugāvagavā’’tyādinā (2-67) karīyamānaṃ gāvādaisādikaṃ, aittha pana napuṃsakai aikavacanantaṃ daṇḍi kulanti vakkhamānattā naudāhaṭaṃ, yausu pana ‘‘yaulaupanisudīghau’’ti (2-88) adīghassāpi dīghavidhānasāmatthiyā paṭhamamaiva dīghassa rassābhāvaikāva kathāti naudāhaṭaṃ, daṇḍīdaṇḍīni ic‍caiva tu bhavati, naussavāti pāṭhau dissati, aittha vij‍jamānai gai parai aukārantassa vā rassāpaj‍janadausampasaṅgatau naussā’ti pāṭhaina bhavitabbaṃ|

67. gaussā

gāvaina gavaināti karaṇaipi samānaṃ, gāvassa gavassāti chaṭṭhiyāpi|

69. gavaṃ

saināti sutattā saiiti vuttiyaṃ nimittaṃ parikappitaṃ|

72. gāvu

saddantarattaina kauci dausauti sambandhau, ādisitabbā ādaisā kātabbā|

73. yaṃ

paina  pasañ‍ñāya yuttā īpī, paca sau īcāti vāpī abhaidaggahaṇaina, añ‍ñassāti ikāra ukāraūkārassa|

77. smi

laukai daivadattau dattauti aikadaisaina samudāyavauhāra dassanatau sminautīmassa smiṃ vacanassātyayamatthau dassitautyāha-‘smiti iminā’tiādi|

81. jhalā

brahmādīsu ‘itau k‍vaci sassa ṭānubandhau’ti pāṭhāti sambandhau, brahmādīsūti ‘‘ghabrahmāditai’’ti (2-60) suttai vuttabrahmādīsu, pāṭhāti ‘itau’tyādinau gaṇasuttassa pāṭhā, ṭānubandhauti iminā (aiṭādaisaṃ) niddisati|

85. kū

sau bhasmā antau yassa sau tadantau samudāyau, tassa, ‘‘cānukaḍḍhitaṃ nauttaratrānuvattatī’’ti viñ‍ñūvacanaṃ, vidunau vidūti kūpac‍cayantassa udāharaṇaṃ, viñ‍ñunau viñ‍ñūti ‘‘vitau ñātau’’ti (5-39) sabbañ‍ñunau sabbañ‍ñūti ‘‘kammā’’ti (5-40)|

86. lau

laupautvaiva siddhattāti vaumuttapakkhai laupautīmināva siddhattā|

87. nanau

amuyāti itthiyaṃ yādaisau, nanautīmassidaṃ pac‍cudāharaṇaṃ|

88. yaulau

paṭhamādutiyāyaunamaikatau udāharaṇāni aṭṭhī aṭṭhīniti, tānica yaulaupanilātissaiva dassitāni, tathāyaivāñ‍ñānipi ñāyantīti|

92. ntassa

ṭamuttapakkhauti  asantaṃ kubbantassāti ṭādaisaina muttapakkhau,ntussacaivanti pasiddhattāva ṭādaisamuttapakkhaṃ upamaiti, ‘‘sañ‍jātaṃ tārakāditvitau’’ti (5-45) itau andhitā|

93. yausu

ṭādaisau-tippasaj‍jatīti kasmā vuttaṃ nanu tadantassa samudāyassa pattai ‘‘chaṭṭhiyantassā’’ti (1-17) antassaiva hauti, tathāsati naivātippasaṅgau, tasmā kasmātippasaj‍jatīti vuttanti, sac‍caṃ, tathāpi ‘jhissā’ti vattuṃ sak‍kuṇaiyyattai issāti vuttatta vuttanthi sāmatthiyatau tadantassa ṭādaisau-tippasaj‍jatīti tathā vuttanti, nanu ‘jhissā’ti vuttaipi sauvātippasaṅgau tadavatthauyaivāti sac‍caṃ, tathāpi yadi samudāyassa ṭādaisau-bhimatau siyā ‘tathā sati kiṃ jhaggahaṇaina issatvaiva vadaiyyāti nātippasaj‍jatīti, jhaggahaṇai nāmantai ikārassaiva jhasañ‍ñattāti idaṃ pana visaisanattaina vattu manicchitai yuj‍jati|

94. vaivau

sahacaritañāyassāti ‘‘taṃsahacaritā taggahaṇaina gayhantī’’ti ñāyassa|

95. yaumhi

haitukurusaddaihi yausu iminā ṭādaisai ‘‘laupau’’tīminā yaulaupai vā sampattai ‘‘pac‍cayanissitaṃ bahiraṅga’’nti yaulaupatau ‘‘pakatinissitamantaraṅga’’nti ‘‘antaraṅgabahiraṅgasu antaraṅgavidhi balavā’’ti paṭhamaṃ ṭādaisai nimittābhāvāva laupā bhāvauti dassaitumāha-‘haitukurusaddaihi’c‍cādi|

99. sabbā

nanu katarādayauva sabbādisaddavacanīyā haunti samāsainābhihitattā, na tu sabbasaddau saddassai (pautthakai) tyāha-‘avayavainai’tyādi,yadipi ‘sabba saddau  ādi yaisa’nti avayavaina viggahau, tathāpi samudāyauvāssatthau bhavati… yaisantyanaina samudāyassa parāmāsāti bhāvau, itīti haitumhi, kau bhavantānaṃ dvin‍naṃ bahun‍naṃ vā aikau daiva dattau kaṭhādīhi vā atthai dvīhi bahūhi vā aikassa niddhāraṇai sati kimhā niddhāraṇai ratara ratamaisu ṇādivuttiyaṃ ‘‘aikatthatāya’’nti (2-119) vibhattilaupai ‘‘rānubandhaintasarādissā’’ti (4-132) antasarādilaupai kiṃsaddassa rūpasiddhīti dassaitumāha katamā’tiādi, ubhau aṃsā assāti atthai ‘‘ayubhadvitīhaṃsai’’ti (4-49) ayai‘‘sarau laupau sarai’’ti (1-26) saralaupai rūpasiddhīti āha-‘ubhau aṃsā’ic‍cādi, kāyaṃ vavatthā nāmai tyāha-‘sābhidhaiyyā’tyādi, avadhic‍cādinā tassaivātthaṃ vibhāvaiti, adhaṃsau abhaṭṭhatā avadhibhāvaiyaivāvaṭṭhānaṃ, sābhidhaiyyā paikkhāti aittha sau sakau abhidhaiyyau atthau disādi sābhidhaiyyauti gahitauti āha-‘pubbādīna’ntiādi, sābhidhaiyyanti kammatau parā apapubbā ‘ikkha=dassanai’tīmasmā ‘‘k‍vacaṇa’’iti (5-41) aṇpac‍cayai sābhidhaiyyāpaikkhau, sau vāvadhiniyamauti dassaitumāha-‘tamapaikkhati’c‍cādi, tatthādhippāyaṃ vivarati ‘tathāhi’c‍cādinā, aittha ca parādisaddābhidhaiyyau-tthau disādaisādi pubbādisaddābhidhaiyyassātthassa disāya daisādinau vāvadhibhāvaina niyamavasaina pavattati, yau cāvadhibhāvau niyamavasaina pavattati, saucāvadhibhāvau niyamavasaina pavattau pubbādisaddavacanīyaṃ disādaisādi mapaikkhatīti sābhidhaiyyāpaikkhauvadhiniyamauyaiva vavatthāti vuc‍catītyayamadhippāyau, na tu abhidhaiyyāyanti asambhavā, asambhavamaiva dassaintau āha-‘yau hi’c‍cādi, kausal‍laina nimittaināti kusala saddassa pavattinimittaina kausal‍laina, uttarākuravau uttarakurudīpa vāsinau, daisau vā, ‘nāñ‍ñañ‍ca nāmappadhānā’’ti (2-136) anainaiva sañ‍ñāyaṃ sabbādikāriyassa nisiddhattai kimasañ‍ñāyaṃbhyanainaityāha ‘nāñ‍ñañ‍cai’tyādi, kiñ‍ci pubbattādikamuddissa kaisañ‍ci pubbauparautyādikā atthānugatā sañ‍ñā anvatthasañ‍ñā|

104.ti

‘‘vānaikañ‍ñatthai’’ti (3-17)  vikappānuvattanatau ayuttattābhāvaṃ dassaiti‘nayidamayutta’nti, yuttatā cāssa pāṭhai byabhicāra dassanatauti vattumāha-‘napuṃsakai’c‍cādi, pāṭhānugatā hi suttaracanā, vikappānuvattiyā anapuṃsakamidamaiva vacanaṃ tassa k‍vaci byabhicārattai ñāpakanti vattumāha-‘tassa cā’tiādi, avassamaivamaittha atthau gahaitabbau, añ‍ñathā yathāvaṭṭhitapāṭhānuk‍kamainātthaggahaṇai sati suttassa suttassā (pautthakai) mukhyattappatītitau kathantamañ‍ñassa ñāpakaṃ bhavaiyya, mukhyabhāvai hi ñāpakatthaṃ na yuj‍jaiyya|

108. atai

nanu ca ‘‘lakkhaṇapaṭipadavuttaisu paṭipadavuttassaiva gahaṇa’’ntīmāya paribhāsāya lakkhaṇikassākārassa gahaṇaṃ na pappautītyāsaṅkiya paṭipādayamāha-‘rassākārai’c‍cādi, paribhāsāya cassāyamatthau ‘‘lakkhīyatainainaiti lakkhaṇaṃ, sādhvasādhujānana haisuttā sutta sattha muc‍catai, paṭigatampadaṃ paṭipadaṃ, uc‍cāritamañ‍jasaityatthau, lakkhaṇañ‍ca paṭipadañ‍ca lakkhaṇapaṭipadāni, taihi vuttāni rūpāni lakkhaṇapaṭipadavuttāni, taisu, vuttasaddau pac‍caikamparisamāpīyatai ‘lakkhaṇavuttaisu paṭipadavuttaisū’ti, tatrāñ‍casā yamaniddiṭṭhaṃ, kaivalaṃ lakkhaṇadassanaināsmiṃ lakkhaṇai satyavassamanaina rūpaina bhavitabbanti lakkhaṇaina paṭipāditaṃ, taṃ lakkhaṇavuttamityuc‍catai, yantvañ‍jasā saddainaiva paṭipāditaṃ, taṃ paṭipadavuttanti vuc‍catai, tasmiṃ lakkhaṇavuttai paṭipadavuttai ca rūpai sati paṭipadavuttassaiva gahaṇaṃ, naitarassai’’ti, paṭipadavuttassaivaityavadhāraṇaina niyamarūpabhāssā paribhāsāyāvagamyatai, sau ca niyamau aniyamapubbakai sandaihai satyavatiṭṭhatai, tasmā yatra lakkhaṇavuttamaiva kaivalaṃ, na paṭipadavuttaṃ, tatra nāyaṃ paribhāsā vatiṭṭhatai, yatrāpi paṭipadavuttamaiva kaivalaṃ, na lakkhaṇavuttaṃ, saupyaitissā avisayau, yatra dvai rūpāni sambhavanti tatraivā yamparibhāsā sandaihāpākaraṇamukhaina pavattāti ñāyappattauyaivā matthau  imāya paribhāsāyānuvādyatai, tathāhi sandaihaṭhānaisu yadyapyaikarūpamaivamabhin‍namubhin‍naṃ, tathāpi lakkhaṇavuttaṃ rūpaṃ lakkhaṇai naun‍nīyamānaṃ dhūmaggi viya jalabalākā viya cānu mānikaṃ, yantu paṭipada vuttaṃ rūpaṃ, taṃ pac‍cakkhasiddhaṃ, pac‍cakkhānumānaisu ca pac‍cakkhambalavantaraṃ pamāṇanti atau paṭipadavuttamaiva gayhatīti|

110. k‍vacai

napuṃsakaliṅgai aikāraina na bhavitabbanti sambandhau|

114. laupau

akārassantaraṅgattai kāraṇamāha-‘pakatinissitattā’ti, pakatiyā idaṃ pākataṃ ‘‘ṇau’’ti (4-34) ṇau, laupassa bahiraṅgattai kāraṇamāha-‘pac‍cayanissitattā’ti, antaraṅgaic‍cādinā ‘‘antaraṅgabahiraṅgaisvantaraṅgavidhi balavā’’ti paribhāsamupalakkhaiti, aṅgasaddau-vayavavācī, antai saddau sattamyatthai, aṅgai bhavamantaraṅgaṃ vibhatthyatthai-saṅkhyasamāsau ‘‘aiaunama vaṇṇai’’ti (1-37) attaṃ rāgamau, aṅgatau bahi bahiraṅgaṃ ‘‘payyapābahitirau puraipacchā vā pañ‍camyā’’ti (3-5) asaṅkhyasamāsau, atra cāntaraṅgaṃ bahiraṅganti nāṅgaibhavamantaraṅgaṃ, apitvantaraṅganissitaṃ kāriyamantaraṅgaṃ, nāpyaṅgatau bahibhūtau samudāyau bahiraṅgaṃ, kintu bahiraṅganissitaṃ kāriyambahiraṅgaṃ yathā mañ‍canissitaisu saddāyantaisu ‘mañ‍cā saddāyantī’tyuc‍catai taṃ viyaiti atauyaivā saṅkhyasamāsaipi liṅgavibhattivacanantarayaugau ‘antaraṅgau antaraṅgā’ti, saṅkaitikau hyayamasaṅkhyasamāsau, na pubbapadatthappadhānau, yathā-pac‍cakkhassākkhanissitattāvabaudhatthaṃ ‘akkhamakkhampati vattatai’ti vicchāya-masaṅkhyasamāsaipi katai pac‍cakkha ñāṇassākkhanissitattañāpanatthaṃ saṅkaitavasaina katau-yamasaṅkhya samāsau, na pubbapadatthappadhānauti‘pac‍cakkhau pac‍cakkhā’ti liṅgavibhatti vacanantarayaugau, tathaivāya-mantaraṅganissitau-yaṃ samudāyanissitauti ñāpanatthaṃ saṅkaitavasaina katau-yamasaṅkhyasamāsauti liṅgavibhattivacanantarayaugau na virujjhatai, athavā antaramaṅgamassa kāriyassātthīti ‘‘saddhāditva’’ (4-84) tathā bahiraṅganti, antaraṅgañ‍ca bahiraṅgañ‍ca  antaraṅgabahiraṅgāni, taisvantaraṅgau balavāti| antaraṅga kāriyassa balavattaṃ sādhaituṃ ‘sabbapaṭhamaṃ vidhaiyyattā’tiādi vuttaṃ, idampana antaraṅgakāriyassa balavabhāvasādhanai kāraṇaṃ na hauti, antaraṅgattā balavabhāvauyaiva hi sabbapaṭhamaṃ vidhaiyyatta kāraṇaṃ, tathā cāha-‘yau laupatau akārassa paṭhamamaiva bhavanai kāraṇamāha antaraṅgattā akārassā’ti, tasmā nāyaṃ pāṭhau ghaṭatai, antaraṅgabalavabhāvasādhanassa pana laukatau icchitattā ayamaivatthai pāṭhau ghaṭatai’’antaraṅgamaiva kāriyambalavaṃ laukai tathādiṭṭhattā, tathā hi laukau pātauvā’’tyādi, laukai tathādiṭṭhattāti antaraṅgabalavabhāvassa laukai aivaṃ vakkhamānanayaina diṭṭhattā, athavā kainaci vidhurappac‍cayaupanipātaina kāraṇavai kal‍laina vā kadāci kattumasak‍kuṇaiyyattaipi antaraṅgassa avassaṃ vidhaiyyattaṃ dassaitumāha sabbapaṭhamaṃ vidhaiyyattā’ti, satisambhavaiti nimittassānupahatattā kāriyassa sambhavai sati|

116. yai

ivaṇṇassāti vuttattāti ‘yai passivaṇṇassā’’ti aittha ivaṇṇassāti vuttattā|

118. asaṃ

liṅgavacanabhaidaipi byayarahitattā abyayavantaupasaṅganipātānaṃ pubbācariyasañ‍ñā, asatī saṅkhyā yaisantyānyasaṅkhyānītyāha- ‘aikattai’c‍cādi, aikānaikaisūti vuttattā saṅkhyāvisaisai vidhīyamānāsyādayau kathamasaṅkhyaihi uppaj‍jitumussahantai ic‍cāsayaināha- ‘kathamasaṅkhyaihi syādīnaṃ sambhavau’ti| atha nissaṅkhyaihi hautusyādīnamasambhavau uc‍caṃ rukkhassic‍cādautu saṅkhyāsambhavai bhavitabbamaiva syādīhic‍cāha-‘saṅkhyāsambhavaivai’c‍cādi| vuttiganthassādhippāyaṃ vivarati ‘añ‍ñathai’ tyādinā, kimaisamuppattiyampayaujanaṃ yaināsaṅkhyaihi taduppatti nau-numīyatai c‍cāsaṅkiyāha-‘tassañ‍ca satiya’ntyādi, asati hi syādīnamuppattiyamuc‍camādīnamasaṅkhyānampadattaṃ natthīti yau ādīsu padasmā paraisaṃ tumhāmhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ vaunauādikaṃ padanimittaṃ  kāriyaṃ na siyāti ‘uc‍caṃ vau, uc‍caṃ nau’tiādayau payaugā na sijjhanti, sati tu syāduppattiyaṃ luttaisupi taisu padattanipphattiyāyaiva taduppattitau padattai siddhai padalakkhaṇaṃ kāriyaṃ sijjhataivāti bhāvau| paraihi aittha ‘tassaṃ sālāya’nti vacanicchāyaṃ ‘tatra sālāya’nti yathā siyāti ‘‘abyayatvā syādinau’’ti (pā, 2-4-82) abyayatau parassitthiyamāppac‍cayassāpi laupau vihitau, taināha- ‘asaṅkhyaihi’c‍cādi, tadayuttanti āppac‍cayassa laupavidhāna mayuttaṃtyatthau| atha kainātra āppac‍cayau vidhīyatai yaināssāpi laupau-nusāsīyatai ‘‘itthiyamatvā’’ti (3-26) cai naitadatthi, itthiyamāppac‍cayassa vidhānatau-saṅkhyānañ‍cāliṅgattā, tasmā asaṅkhyaihi āppac‍cayassa sambhavauyaiva natthītyanatthakamāppac‍cayassa laupavacananti nānatthakaṃ itthiyanti (3-26) hi guṇippadhānau niddaisau itthattavati āppac‍cayādayau vidhīyantai ‘tassaṃ sālāya (mic‍cai tasmiṃ padatthai) tatrai’ti asaṅkhyamidamitthattavati vattatai, tasmā siyāyai vaitasmā āppac‍cayautyavassamāppac‍cayassa laupau vidhaiyyauti yau mañ‍ñatai, tampati āha-‘yadīpi’c‍cādi, visaisābhidhānātyupagamai vatta mānauyamasaṅkhyasamudāyau‘niddaisau vattatai’ti caubhayamikkhatai, itthiyanti niddaisassa guṇippadhānattaṃ sādhaituṃ ‘itthattavati hi āppac‍cayādayau’tiāha, tatraitīmassāsaṅkhyassa sālāyaṃ vattanatau āha- ‘itthattavati cā’ti, ayampasiddhirūpaina na vattataiti sambandhau, yadi ayampasiddhirūpaina vattatai, kutau carahi itthattāvasāyautyāha- ‘itthattāvasāyau’c‍cādi| idāni jinindabuddhivacanamānīya tassā yuttattamubbhāvayitumāha- ‘yaupyāhi’c‍cādi, yadīpic‍cādi jinindabuddhivacanaṃ, tassāti itthattassa, padatthau ca itthattaṃ siyāti yaupyāhaiti sambandhau, gammamānatthattā itisaddāppayaugau, tassāyuttanti tassaivaṃ vādinau jinindabuddhinau vacanamayuttaṃtyatthau, kutauc‍cāha- ‘aivaṃ hi’c‍cādi, yadi itthattassa vākyatthatā itthippac‍cayanivattikatā, aivaṃ sati sālāyamic‍cādisaddantarāpaikkhā itthattappatīti tatraityādīsu siyāti atthau, naiva tathā patīyatīti bhāvau, vuttamaivatthaṃ phuṭayitumāha- ‘tathāhi’c‍cādi, aviddasuayanti diṭṭhantau pañ‍ñāsau, aviddasu  ayanti ubhayatrāpyatraikavacanasissa nivattiyā kataṃ yamaikattaṃ, taṃ saddantarādyanapaikkhamaiva patīyataityatthau, itthatta vuttinā sālādisaddaina sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ tatraityaitassāti aitāvatā āppac‍cayau uppaj‍jatīti ca na sak‍kā vattunti dassaintau āha-‘samānādhikaraṇattampi’c‍cādi, samānādhikaraṇattampīti (itthi) vuttinā sālādisaddaina sāmānādhikaraṇyampi, āppac‍cayādīnaṃ nāṅganti sambandhau|

119. aika

aivāti anuvattataiaiva, aikautthau yassa pakatyādisamudāyassa sau aikatthau ‘assa caikatthasaddassa pavattinimittaṃ īyādi vidhānantyāha- ‘aikatthatā īyādividhāna’nti, īyādivuttiyanti vattanaṃ vutti īyādīnaṃ vutti, īyādividhānai satityatthau, samāsatauti samāsitā, tainaivāti ‘‘dissantañ‍ñaipi pac‍cayā’’ti (4-120) suttainaiva|

120. pubba

yadā cātrādhisaddappayaugau tadā tainaiva vibhatyatthassa vuttattā na sattamī payuj‍jatai, yadā sattamī, na tadādhisaddau, tainā sakapadainaiva viggahau aivaṃ dassanīyauti āha- ‘itthīsu kathā pavattā’ti, itthisaddatau sattamībhavanai kāraṇamāha- ‘taṃsamānādhikaraṇattā’ti, taina adhisaddaina samānatthattāti atthau|

121. nātau

sau ca amādaisau, ādaisassaivāti aṃādaisassaiva, apañ‍camiyāti pañ‍camiṃ vaj‍jaitvā| añ‍ñatra vidhīyamānau-yamamādaisau pañ‍camiyampaṭisiddhattā pañ‍camiyā paṭisaidhaina sayampi paṭisiddhau nāma hautītyāha ‘ādaisassaivāyaṃ paṭisaidhau’ti, laupapaṭisaidhassa ‘nātau’ti pubbavākyaina vuttassa ayampaṭisaidhau naiti sambandhau, atha kathamidamapañ‍camyātyamādaisainaivātisambandhati na laupa paṭisaidhaināti paribhāsamāha-‘anantarassa vidhi vā hauti paṭisaidhau  vā’ti, aittha antarasaddassānaikatthattaipīhāntarāḷavācīti na vij‍jatai antarammajjhamassaityanantarau, antarāḷamattapaṭisaidhai payaujanaṃ natthītyantarāḷagatassa vidhinau paṭisaidhassa cābhāvānantarautyupa carīyatai anantarā gāmātyādi viya, diṭṭhattai pana majjhassa na vij‍jatai-ntara maisanti viggahai suttaipyanantarassa vidhānantarassāpi diṭṭhattā dvin‍namaikaupyanantarautyuc‍catai, tainaivānantarassāti paribhāsāyamaika vacanaṃ, itisaddau idamatthai, iti imāya paribhāsāyādaisainābhisambandhatītyatthau, taināti yathāvuttaparibhāsāya vasaina, yaina kāraṇainādaisainābhisambandhati tainātyatthau, alaupauti laupa paṭisaidhau, sāmatthiyāladdhanti dvin‍namatthānaṃ vidhānāya dvai vākyāni bhavanti, tatra ‘nātau’tīdaṃ paṭisaidhavidhāyakamaikaṃ vākyaṃ, ‘amapañ‍camiyā’ti dutiyamamādaisassa, tatrāpañ‍camiyātyādaisainaivāti sambandhati, vuttavidhānā na laupapaṭisaidhaina, tathāsatyamapañ‍camiyātidaṃ vākyaṃ yadi na visaisatthamālambatai tamantarainānupapattitau-ñ‍ñathā kintyanainātthabalaina laddhanti atthau, alaupauti jautaitīti sambandhau, vākyabhaidaina vivaraṇaṃ kataṃ, na aikavākyattaināti byatiraikau aikavākyatāyañhi kāriya dvayassapi pañ‍camiyampaṭisaidhau siyā ‘pañ‍camiṃ vaj‍jaitvā taṃ kāriyadvayaṃ vaiditabba’nti, yadipi siddhāti sambandhau, yaina nāppattiyātyanaina ‘‘yaina nāppattai vidhirārabbhatai, tassa bādhanambhavatī’’ti mamparibhāsamupalakkhaiti, kimatthamihāyaṃ paribhāsā paṭhyatai iha satthai-pavādā ussaggai bādhantaiti niyamau, tatra cussaggā duvidhā niyatappattayau aniyatappattayau caiti, yai sabbatthāpavādavisayampavisanti, tai niyatappattayau vuc‍cantai, yai k‍vacidaivāpavādavisayamavagāhantai, tai aniyatappattayau vuc‍cantai, aivaṃ duvidhaisussaggaisu yai-niyatappattayau, taisamaiva bādhanamicchīyatai, na niyatikānaṃ, taina tappadassanatthamayamparibhāsā paṭhyataiti, yainaityussaggassa niddaisau, naiti paṭisaidhavācī nipātau, appattai asamphuṭṭhai visayai ityajjhāhāriyaṃ, yaina ussaggaina nāppattai pattaiaiva samphuṭṭhaiaiva visayaityatthau… ‘dvai paṭisaidhā pākatamatthaṃ gamaintī’ti katvā, vidhīyatīti, paṭisaidhau vidhānañ‍ca, ārabbhatai vidhī

yatai  paṭhyataityatthau, anaināpavādavidhindassaiti, tassaiti ādauyainaiti niddiṭṭhassaussaggassabādhanambhavatyapavādaina, yaina tvappattai visayai ārabbhatai tassa bādhanaṃ na bhavatītyatthau, kutauti cai anavakāsattā, tathāhi yadi ussaggaina sabbau visayau gahitau siyā, kau-pavādassaañ‍ñau visayau siyā, tasmā anavakāsattā (na) apa (vādai) pana tassa bādhanambhavatītyavasaiyaṃ, yaina tussaggaina kauci daivāpavādavisayau-nuppattausiyā, nasabbau, tampati sāvakā sattā tambādhataiti vuttaṃ hauti, ‘pañ‍capūlimānayai’tyatra pañ‍can‍naṃ pūlānaṃ samāhārauti visaisanasamāsau ‘‘nadāditauvī’’ (3-27) samāharaṇaṃ samāhārauti samāhārassapi bhāvarūpattā āha ‘bhāvappadhānattaipī’ti, guṇabhūtadabbāni pūlāni|

125. ima
